## Recurring failure modes (instances of §3–§5, kept for pattern-matching)

The citation-extraction boundary named below is bounded at mechanism grain, not unknown: code inspection finds six non-fixture helpers that mint `DeclarationRef` from caller-supplied module and declaration strings (`wet_surface_ref`, `roster_decl`, `target_decl`, `principle_ref`, `family_subject`, `coverage_subject`). Their calls are not members of the parser-visible 31 and are not silently counted as citation targets: an argument may be computed, so a textual call count is not an unchecked-citation population. Whether the cited-symbol wall reaches those wrappers through another path is unmeasured here; establishing that separate question requires a planted wrapper call and execution, not an inference from this extractor read.

The landing measurement partitions the 31 parser-visible identities into **2 cited-and-syntactically-called** (`gunbc.auth.credentials`, `gunbc.auth.optional_impersonation`) and **29 cited-not-syntactically-called**. It independently partitions them by compile disposition: **25 live under `dag/`, the first and therefore entry-producing root of the existing `--source-root dag --source-root src/v2` corpus compile; 6 live under `src/v2/`, which that invocation indexes only as a dependency pool.** Because this population has no inbound import edges, those six pool-only identities are structurally unreachable by that operation. The call split is evidence, not semantic resolution: indirection may hide a call and an equal bare callee spelling may bind elsewhere. It separates an under-declaration candidate present only in the called arm from the compile-coverage deficit present in both arms. PR #9453 is the worked construction-side repair for one called specimen: the missing import was a real dependency and incidentally makes that citee enter a consumer closure. It does not repair the shared coverage class. The existing compile mechanism covers the 25 `dag/` identities by collecting every `.dag` file under its first source root as an entry regardless of inbound edges; coverage for the six `src/v2/` pool-only identities remains open. The current whole compile-route cost is UNMEASURED: the available 7 GiB figure is a rounded-up, stale lower-bound proxy from the former compile-clean floor route, not a demand measurement, and differencing it from a budget would fabricate a shortfall. The existing carrier names the re-measure trigger: a dated uncensored whole-tree peak on the compile route itself. No citation-derived edge is proposed for the not-called arm: it would manufacture reachability from the claim being checked and make the check green by construction. **THE RED EVIDENCE NO LONGER DEPENDS ON THE LIVE CREDENTIALS DEFECT.** The live 87.712s refusal remains a dated mechanism measurement, but `orphan_entry_compile_has_a_controlled_red_and_clean_control` now compiles two in-memory, caller-authored orphan entries: one undefined value must produce `CompileRefused`, and the clean peer must produce `CompileCompleted`. Repairing or importing any live retained module cannot green that discriminating pair.

**THE THIRD EMIT-STAGE ESCAPE MODE NOW HAS A MEASURED POPULATION (2026-08-27), AND THE NUMBER IS 31, NOT THE 39 A TEXT SCAN SUGGESTED.** `v1_compiler.declaration_index` already derives each module's imports and typed-literal `DeclarationRef` citations from the parse sweep. Its one ingestion-derived join now reports every distinct non-fixture module cited by a different non-fixture module and reached by no import edge, with all 31 RETAINED IDENTITIES beside the count on the required parse phase. Fixture citers and targets are evidence carriers, not production authority demand; self-citation creates no consumer; both are excluded by construction. The earlier regex figure 39 was an instrument result, never an oracle: on the tree measured here its comparable scan returned 38, including six `module_path` fields that are not `DeclarationRef`s at all, while one real citation routed through the local `wet_surface_ref` wrapper lies outside the index's declared extraction boundary. Neither discrepancy is smoothed away. This bounds mode three at the exact grain the parser-backed instrument can establish; it does not bound mode one, all emit-stage diagnostics, or every possible citation spelling. **THE DISCRIMINATING EXECUTION EXISTS AND THE REQUIRED PHASE DOES NOT.** Compiling the retained `gunbc.auth.credentials` entry refused on its four known hard diagnostics in 87.712s; compiling the clean orphan control `extdeps.vendor.arm` passed in 85.119s. That pair proves entry compilation detects the class without making every legitimate orphan red. It also refuses enrollment of a serial 31-entry phase: these subjects cost about 85s each, with 21–24s (about 27%) in reconcile, not the neighbouring emit-compile row's 40–50s and ~85% reconcile over a different roster. Both measurements stand; neither is generalized across the other population. At this cost shape a whole-population serial wall is outside the admitted runner envelope, so mode three remains unprevented while its exact parser-visible remainder is now bounded and named. The construction-side candidate is citation-derived dependency edges, which would make a cited authority enter its citer's closure instead of adding a second compile; it is NOT claimed here because qualified references already prove import closure under-approximates dependency, the index deliberately does not resolve every wrapper-produced `DeclarationRef`, and the closure-growth and historical/quarantined-citation semantics have not been bounded. Its next decision requires those measurements, not a second edge authority improvised in the census.

- **censored estimator drops its own tail** (an estimate of a variable is computed over the observations that SURVIVED a threshold on that same variable, so the sample structurally excludes its own extreme and the estimate is biased low with no bound. It is the neighbour of `instrument_output_read_as_subject_content` moved one step earlier: there a REPORT is complete for the reporter and read as complete for the consumer; here the truncation is in the ESTIMATOR'S OWN DOMAIN, and no amount of reading the instrument's output correctly recovers what the filter removed. SPECIMEN, both directions, 2026-09-01: the required floor's per-run cost inflation was estimated by pairing every claim that reported a cost in BOTH attempts of one tree -- median 1.053, p90 1.107. A claim that exceeds the cpu ceiling goes INTERRUPTED-BEFORE-VERDICT AND REPORTS NO COST, so the pairing dropped exactly the two most inflated observations; recovered from the ceiling itself, their inflation was at least 500/418 = 1.196, censored from above. THE SECOND HALF IS WHY THE ROW EXISTS: a peer lane, correcting a DIFFERENT defect in the same measurement, took the biased p90 by relay and derived an at-risk population of ONE -- an understatement arriving with the credibility of a retraction, which is the artifact nobody re-audits. Both counts were lower bounds and neither was labelled as one. RECOGNITION RULE: whenever an estimate is computed over rows that COMPLETED, RETURNED, PASSED, or were OBSERVED, ask what the incomplete rows would have contributed -- and if the incompleteness is caused by the very variable being estimated, the statistic is a floor and must be reported as one. The tell is a filter and an estimand naming the same quantity: cost estimated over rows that finished, latency over requests that did not time out, size over responses that were not truncated. REMEDY: report the censored bound rather than the sample statistic, and recover the excluded observations from the THRESHOLD they crossed, which is a real datum -- a row killed at 500ms is not missing, it is known to be above 500.)
- **a selection view read as the population** (a decidable predicate is written over MEASURED values -- cost at or above N, latency under N, rows a report printed -- and its output is then treated as the population of the class under study. Membership in that set is a property of THE MEASUREMENT, not of the subject: it moves when the instrument moves, when load moves, when the reporter truncates, while the subject is unchanged. The set and the population are two objects joined by an assumption nobody states, and the assumption is false exactly when the measured quantity carries an unbounded term the threshold cannot see beneath. **WHAT MAKES THIS DIFFERENT FROM ITS NEIGHBOURS IS THE ROUTE IT ARRIVES BY, AND THAT IS ALSO THE WHOLE DIFFICULTY: IT ARRIVES AS THE FIX FOR A REAL OBJECTION.** `instrument_output_read_as_subject_content` is a report read past its own promise, and its closest unnamed relative here is a statistic computed over the observations that SURVIVED a threshold on the very variable being estimated -- filed separately as `censored_estimator_drops_its_own_tail`, and cited here by identity because that row now resolves in this tree; it was carried descriptively while it was unlanded, since a boundary drawn against a symbol that does not resolve is worse than a boundary drawn descriptively. Both of those are errors in the READING. This one is produced BY diligence. The predicate is decidable, it is honestly derived, and it is very often the correct answer to a legitimate refusal -- which is precisely why the next question does not get asked. **BEING CAREFUL IS WHAT MAKES IT INVISIBLE: the diligence is spent on the repair, and the repair's correctness is what stops anyone asking whether it answers the question that was posed.** A defect arriving inside a correct fix is not caught by the diligence that produced the fix. SPECIMEN, WITH BOTH ENDS BELONGING TO THE SAME AUTHOR THREE HOURS APART, which is why it is filed rather than blamed. (i) A peer lane computed a median and p95 over the rows a required-floor run's `[over-cost]` listing PRINTED -- an instrument that ranks by cost and truncates to a fixed head, and whose own closing line says so and names the per-claim cost artifact the run uploads as the complete population. This author correctly told them an order statistic of a truncated tail describes nothing, because the truncation is a property of the REPORTER. THE TWO PRODUCERS ARE NAMED RATHER THAN THEIR OUTPUTS TRANSCRIBED, per DESIGN section 6: the truncated view is that `[over-cost]` listing, and the population is `required_floor_claim_cost.tsv` as uploaded by the same run -- either is re-derivable from any floor run, and no count here needs to be trusted. (ii) The same author then had a declared drop refused for defining its population as `every required row whose headroom is smaller than the observed inflation` while calling that inflation censored with no upper bound -- an undecidable membership predicate, correctly refused under DESIGN section 4b(3). The fix was a decidable admission rule over each run's own per-claim cost artifact, which was the right repair for the refusal, and its output was then written into the row AS THE POPULATION. With an unbounded contention term, no lower threshold can prove the rows beneath it unaffected, so the set was a view the whole time. REPAIR: name the closed subject universe from the SUBJECT rather than from any measurement -- for the specimen, every required identity for which the cpu deadline is armed while it executes under the uncontrolled shared-runner envelope -- and keep the threshold set, which is genuinely useful, under an honest name: an EXPOSED ATTENTION SUBSET for prioritising work. A COROLLARY WORTH CARRYING SEPARATELY, because conflating the two hides the seam: the evidence floor behind such a threshold may be MONOTONE while the membership set is NOT. An observed floor only rises, so a constant derived from it only falls; individual identities still enter and leave the subset as their measured values vary. Monotonicity of the bound says nothing about stability of the set. **RECOGNITION RULE: when a set is produced by comparing a MEASURED value against a threshold, ask what the set would contain if the measurement were taken again under different conditions -- and ask it hardest when the predicate was just written to satisfy a reviewer, because that is the moment the answer feels settled.**)
- **a citation to a symbol that has not landed** (a canonical row cites an identity that exists only in an OPEN PR, so on main -- the tree the row lands in -- the citation resolves to nothing. **THE MECHANISM IS THAT THE TWO STATES ARE INDISTINGUISHABLE AT THE CITING END.** A name that resolves and a name that will resolve are the same text in the same position; nothing at the point of authorship separates them, so this is not carelessness and reading more carefully does not find it. The check that discriminates is a LOOKUP AGAINST MAIN; the check an author naturally performs is a lookup against their own context, in which the symbol is vividly present because they or a peer just wrote it. Both receipts below were caught by CI and neither by review, which is what that asymmetry predicts. **A VOCABULARY DEFECT CARRIES IT BETWEEN LANES, AND THAT HALF IS WHAT MAKES THE CLASS PREVENTABLE RATHER THAN MERELY DETECTABLE.** One receipt arrived through a MESSAGE saying a class was `filed`. That word is true the moment a row is authored in a branch and is heard as `the tree carries it`; the two states collapse into one utterance. So the rule has a speech half: never say FILED without saying filed WHERE -- `authored in gunbc#NNNN, open` or `landed on main` -- because a reader cannot recover the distinction the word destroyed. TWO INDEPENDENT RECEIPTS, ONE HOUR APART, DIFFERENT LANES AND DIFFERENT CARRIERS, EACH CAUGHT BY CI. (i) A recurring-failure-mode row drew its load-bearing boundary against a sibling class that lived in the author's own other open PR. (ii) A proof field in a separate lane cited a class living in a third session's open PR, reached through the `filed` message above. Both authors repaired it the same way without knowing of each other: DESCRIBE THE SIBLING BY SHAPE RATHER THAN NAMING IT. THE RULE, and it is stronger than it first sounds: A CANONICAL ROW MAY CITE ONLY WHAT RESOLVES ON MAIN AT THE MOMENT THE ROW LANDS. Not `resolves eventually`. **That formulation FORBIDS STACKING the citing PR behind the cited one**, which is the tempting repair precisely because it feels like it fixes the citation -- it satisfies resolves-eventually and fails resolves-at-landing, so it defers the defect rather than removing it. THREE ADMISSIBLE FORMS, AND THE RANKING BETWEEN THE FIRST TWO IS CONDITIONAL ON WHETHER THE CITED IDENTITY IS STABLE -- an unconditional ranking was proposed, and withdrawn by its own author on the argument below. WHERE THE CITED IDENTITY IS STABLE, name the PR AND ITS OPENNESS -- `authored as <identity> in gunbc#NNNN, which is open at this writing, so this points at the PR carrying the row rather than asserting an identity that does not yet resolve`. It stays TRUE after the cited PR lands and TIGHTENS to the bare identity rather than needing correction. **WHERE THE CITED IDENTITY IS NOT STABLE, BOUNDARY BY DESCRIPTION RANKS FIRST INSTEAD, AND AN IDENTITY IN AN OPEN PR UNDER ACTIVE REVIEW IS PRECISELY THE UNSTABLE CASE.** If the cited PR RENAMES its row before landing -- under review pressure, or because two lanes converge and merge their classes -- the PR-naming form points at a real PR carrying a row that no longer has that name, AND IT FAILS WHILE STILL LOOKING RESOLVABLE: the PR number resolves, the identity does not, and the sentence reads as correct. A description survives that, because it does not name the thing that changed. So: PR-plus-openness when the identity is settled, description when it is in motion. BOUNDARY BY DESCRIPTION -- name the sibling's shape and not its symbol -- is otherwise second: correct, but it must be REWRITTEN once the sibling lands, and a rewrite is where a citation quietly stops matching the row it describes. Never: a bare identity that does not resolve on main today, which is worse than no citation at all because a reader takes the name as established and stops checking. RUNG, CEILING AND TRIGGER, per DESIGN section 4b. RUNG FOUND AT: MITIGATABLE. The repository already runs a required cited-symbol resolution check -- its rung drop `cited_symbol_census` is RETIRED, so the capability is live -- and it did not fire on either receipt, because BOTH CITATIONS WERE PROSE INSIDE AN `authored: String`, not typed references. A prose name is not reachable from the namespace tree the check walks, so the check was not weak here; it was not applicable. What caught both was a review bot reading the text, which is containment after the fact and not prevention. ATTAINABLE CEILING, AND IT IS NOT STRUCTURAL WHILE THE CITATION STAYS PROSE: deciding whether an identity-shaped token inside a paragraph is a CITATION or merely a mention is not decidable, so a scanner over prose would be a heuristic, and DESIGN section 5 forbids dressing one as a wall. This class therefore sits in the *outside the modeled guarantee* column for as long as row-to-row citation is prose -- observed and repaired by reading, never gated -- which is the same standing `unbacked_execution_claim` records for the same reason. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, A CAPABILITY AND NOT AN ARTIFACT: A TYPED ROW-TO-ROW CITATION CARRIER -- the ability to express `this row's boundary is drawn against THAT row` as a reference the namespace authority resolves, rather than as a name inside a paragraph. The moment such a citation is typed, the EXISTING required check covers it and the class climbs to structurally guaranteed for every typed citation in one step, with no new gate to build. `RecurringFailureMode` carries an `evidence: List<DeclarationRef>` field, which is the nearest existing carrier and is NOT that capability: it records declarations that witness the class, not the class's boundary against a sibling, and pressing it into service would be a second meaning for one field. WHY THIS ROW'S OWN `evidence` IS EMPTY, stated rather than left to look like an omission: both receipts are PULL REQUESTS AND REVIEW ARTIFACTS, not declarations, so there is no `DeclarationRef` to carry them and the field is honestly empty rather than padded. THE RECEIPTS AT CHECKABLE GRAIN: (i) gunbc#9946, review 58237 -- the boundary clause of `selection_view_read_as_population` named `censored_estimator_drops_its_own_tail`, which at that moment lived only in the same author's other open PR, gunbc#9932. BOTH IDENTITIES RESOLVE IN THIS TREE NOW, which is why they are named here rather than described: the receipt is that they did NOT resolve when the citation was written, and the repair the citing row carries is the descriptive-then-tightened form this row ranks second; (ii) gunbc#9949, review 58260 -- a proof field naming a class living in a third session's open PR, gunbc#9939, reached through the `filed` message. Both reviews are codex, both on the first pushed head of their PR, and each names the unresolvable identity explicitly. **THE TWO RECEIPTS ARE CARRIED IN TWO DIFFERENT ADMISSIBLE FORMS, AND THAT IS DELIBERATE: the first names identities because its two subjects have since landed, the second names a PR and its openness because gunbc#9939 has not. A reader who sees the ranking stated learns the conclusion; a reader who sees one paragraph use both forms because its two cited PRs are in different states learns the DISCRIMINATOR.** A COROLLARY THAT THIS ROW ITSELF DEMONSTRATES, and it is the reason the first receipt does not read as two bare names: WHEN A CITATION TIGHTENS FROM DESCRIPTION TO IDENTITY, THE RECEIPT USUALLY LIVES IN THE STATE THAT JUST ENDED, so tightening SILENTLY DESTROYS IT. This row's first receipt is the historical NON-RESOLUTION of those two names, not the names; tightened to identities alone it would show a reader two symbols that resolve and no evidence the class ever occurred -- a row about unresolvable citations, evidenced by resolvable ones. So a tightening must carry the ended state as a stated fact beside the new name, never merely replace it. THE CAUSE IS A SEPARATE SHAPE AND IS DELIBERATELY NOT FOLDED IN HERE: a correct decomposition -- splitting two classes into two PRs because a rider in a large PR gets skimmed rather than read -- is often what SEVERS the citation path between the pieces. That is a cause held at one receipt and not yet filed; this row is about why the severance is invisible from the citing end, which is a different question with a different repair. If both are ever filed, the causal link is named rather than merged.)
- hollow alias (minimality ≠ grounding)
- state-space conflation (an `Option`/`None` meaning >2 things — split into named variants; its most-repeated form here is **not-applicable rendered as malformed** — one reason symbol over "the input is wrong" and "this strategy had nothing to say about it", which have opposite owners and opposite repairs. Found three times in three stages by one lane (#8801 and #8828 in body lowering, `parse_g0_tokens_remain` in parse), always by reading the producer and never from the message. Recognition rule: if the arm sits downstream of a search, lookup or alternative that returned `Absent`, it is not-applicable and needs its own reason. A SECOND FORM, which does not match that shape and is the same class: **a dichotomy stated over a domain with three states.** Specimen (gunbc#9324): the `floor_resource_sample` comment documented `pswpin` rising with `pgmajfault` as swap and `pgmajfault` rising with `pswpin` flat as mapping churn — two arms over three states, since BOTH FLAT is quiet. Churn is a defect this lane owns and quiet is the absence of one, so the missing arm inverts the verdict. The mechanism of the misread is what the recognition rule keys on: `pgmajfault rises` and `pswpin flat` are two conditions and only their CONJUNCTION is churn, so a reader matching on the cheaper condition alone selects churn for a state that satisfies `pswpin flat` and nothing else — which zero-and-zero does. **Recognition rule for this form: for every arm of a stated dichotomy, enumerate the domain and check that each arm's conditions are required jointly.** What made it invisible rather than merely wrong is that BOTH readers landed on the same arm — 26 intervals so classified by one and 6 by another, neither having compared notes — and agreement reads as confirmation. THAT IS A PROMPT TO RE-DERIVE, NOT A DIAGNOSTIC, and the distinction is load-bearing: convergent readings are equally produced by shared assumptions, a common heuristic, ambiguity in the subject, or one reader having anchored on the other, and n=2 on one specimen cannot separate those from a defect in the rule. What survives is only the weaker direction — agreement between readers of one rule is not independent evidence about that rule, because the shared input is a shared potential defect, so it licenses re-deriving from the domain and never a conclusion about which cause produced it.) **A THIRD FORM, and the one that is hardest to rank because nothing is wrong today: A STATE THAT IS ALREADY LOAD-BEARING AND CARRIED AS AN AD-HOC SPELLING RATHER THAN A CONSTRUCTOR.** Specimen (2026-09-01): the identity key threaded through v1.compiler.coercion has THREE inhabitants -- a real declaring module path, the synthetic <kernel:NAME> that v1.std.core kernel_span mints for kernel nodes, and the empty string meaning unknown -- while every consumer discriminates only empty from non-empty. lookup_checkpoint and type_realization_decision therefore read <kernel:String> as a KNOWN declaration and proceed to the spelling-keyed arm; decl_file_declares_structurally compares that synthetic identity against a roster of real paths with contains, which no <kernel:NAME> can ever match, so the structural gate is unreachable for a kernel-resolved reference BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by decision. The key reaches those gates with exactly that value through type_reference_decl_file, from v1.compiler.emit coerce_primitive_type and v1.compiler.emit_rust rust_named_type_base and rust_applied_type_base. **What makes this the third form rather than an instance of the first two is the evidence that the state is REAL: the tree already handles it, three times, by string prefix.** numeric_realization_declaring_modules carries the literal "<kernel:" as a row in a roster of MODULE PATHS; is_kernel_minted_file is a substring predicate over the same spelling; and v1.compiler.env resolved_node_is_kernel_identity_for_name owns the same fact by EXACT EQUALITY, its own note recording that a substring or prefix test is a second, weaker authority for it. **Recognition rule for this form: when a value's domain is carried in a type that cannot express one of its inhabitants, look for the inhabitant being recognised by SPELLING somewhere -- a prefix test, a magic string in a roster of real values, a sentinel. Each such site is a consumer that already knows the state exists, and their number is the count of authorities the missing constructor has spawned.** NO WRONG ANSWER IS CLAIMED FOR THE SPECIMEN AND NONE WAS MEASURED: for the kernel names reaching those gates today the spelling-keyed answer is the correct one, which is exactly why it never surfaced -- the class is filed on the state space, not on an output, and a form that produces no incident is the one that will be found last. **CLIMB RECEIPT (2026-09-02), appended rather than replacing the filing above, because DESIGN section 4b(4) keeps a class's discriminating evidence enrolled AFTER the climb rather than dissolving it with the production machinery.** The missing constructor now exists: std.coercion TypeDeclarationProvenance is CorpusDeclared | KernelMinted | DeclarationIdentityAbsent, minted by v1.std.core declaration_provenance_of, which decides the kernel arm by EXACT EQUALITY against kernel_span -- the minter itself -- rather than by a prefix test over its output. All four spelling authorities this row counted are gone: the literal "<kernel:" row is deleted from numeric_realization_declaring_modules, is_kernel_minted_file is deleted, and the two gates that substring-compared a synthetic identity against real paths now take the coproduct, so the structural gate's unreachability for a kernel-resolved reference stops being BY CONSTRUCTION and becomes BY DECISION -- a named arm answering false. **WHAT IS NOT CLAIMED, because this row's own honesty is the thing being climbed:** no wrong answer was found before and none is fixed now; every answer is preserved bit for bit, which is why the climb is in the STATE SPACE and not in an output. The empty-string inhabitant is likewise gone from the channel, and the bare-table answer it used to reach silently is now the named ground GroundedByUnidentifiedBareRow, so a declared rung drop that was previously indistinguishable from a properly-keyed answer is countable and refusable. **THE RECOGNITION RULE IS CORROBORATED AND THE CORROBORATION IS INDEPENDENT.** gunbc.type_reference_decl_file_occurrence_census reached this same four-authority set from the opposite direction -- that census asked what each of thirty-nine occurrences NEEDS, this row asked what the identity key can HOLD -- and the counts agreed. **WHAT SURVIVES UNCLIMBED, stated so the row is not read as closed:** CorpusDeclared still carries a file path matched by contains, so the surviving arm answers WHAT A DECLARATION REALIZES AS positionally. v1.compiler.infer_env global_bare_fallback_invariant already states the terminal key for it -- filepaths are irrelevant, the declared module path is the containment tree -- and re-keying that arm needs a declaration reference at each gate, which is a separate change.

**A FOURTH FORM, MEASURED 2026-09-02 (quick-crab-396), AND IT SITS IN THE INTERPRETER RATHER THAN IN ANY MODEL: DECLARED OPTIONAL AND CONSTRUCTED ABSENT ARE TWO STATES WEARING ONE SPELLING.** A record field declared `T?` is read by authors -- and by this document's own §2 reasoning about optional carriers -- as optional-with-a-none-default. It is not. A literal that OMITS the field typechecks, and a later read of that field REFUSES at evaluation with `NoSuchField { type_name, field }`. So `T?` means mandatory-at-construction-and-typed-optional, and the two states the spelling conflates are `the field is absent because the author wrote none` and `the field is absent because the literal predates it`. EVIDENCE, a two-row probe with a positive control, which is what makes it a finding rather than a conjecture about one type: `type Widget { name: String  tag: Int? }` with `Widget { name: "w" }` refuses on `.tag` with `NoSuchField { type_name: "Widget", field: "tag" }`, while `Widget { name: "w", tag: Present { value: 3 } }.tag` reads back correctly -- so the mechanism is neither `reads fail` nor `this type is broken`, it is omission-with-no-default, and it is GENERAL to any record type. RUNG: this is FAIL-CLOSED and explicitly NOT silent wrongness -- the interpreter refuses rather than answering the false that a reader would expect -- which is the whole of the good news and it is what sets the rung at mitigatable rather than below the ladder. WHAT THE CORPUS CURRENTLY BELIEVES INSTEAD, and this is the reason the row exists: two carriers filed this on 2026-08-27 as `a nested compile is not evaluable in the interpreter, refusing with NoSuchField Node.ident` -- v1.tests.claim.reference_derived_disposition_census_witness_test and a sibling paragraph in v1.compiler.emit_rust. Nested compiles are one POPULATION that happens to construct Nodes through helpers omitting v1.std.core Node's `ident` and then read it; they are not the class, and the repair that description suggests (route around the nested pipeline with a host builtin) does not touch the cause. **POPULATION: UNENUMERATED, and stated as such rather than guessed.** It is every record type in the corpus carrying an optional field together with a literal that omits it; `v1.std.core Node.ident` is one specimen and the probe above is another. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, a capability and not an artifact: construction that supplies every declared field, so that a literal omitting an optional field is either unwritable or carries the absent value -- at which point the distinction between the two absences is made at construction and no read can encounter a field that was never written. NOT REPAIRED HERE BY RULING: a defaulting change alters evaluation semantics corpus-wide and needs its own subject, population and review. **A FIFTH FORM, MEASURED 2026-09-03, AND IT IS THE FIRST ONE WHERE THE DECLARATION ALREADY EXISTED: ONE SENTINEL CARRYING TWO FACTS WHILE A TYPED DECLARATION SAYS THEY ARE DISTINCT.** std.algebra free_monoid_collection_templates declares first and last as [ReceiverSelf] -> OptionalOf { inner: ReceiverElement }, and positional get as [ReceiverSelf, Int] -> the same. So the result is an optional OF THE ELEMENT: where the element type is itself optional the two absences are distinct levels. The v1 interpreter's arm bodies returned items.front().cloned().unwrap_or(Value::Null), collapsing 'the collection had no first element' and 'the first element is itself absent' into one Value::Null, because in the interpreter Null is both the absent-marker and a legal element value. DISCRIMINATING RED, executed on both arms, not asserted: over [none, Present { value: 2 }] -- a list of length TWO whose first element is absent -- the interpreter took the OUTER Absent arm (answering 'the list is empty') while emitted Rust answered Present { Absent }. Positive control [5,6].first() returned 5 in both. WHAT MAKES THIS FORM DIFFERENT FROM THE PRECEDING FOUR, and why it is filed as a receipt on this row rather than as its own class: THE MISSING CONSTRUCTOR WAS NOT MISSING. The declaration existed, was mirrored into the seed as std_algebra.rs, and was READ BY THE TYPE CHECKER -- v1.compiler.infer_method walks algebra_templates_for_profile matching on name, which is why std.target_representation representation_spelling_for typechecks with `-> RepresentationSpelling<R>?` returning `|> first` directly. The EVALUATOR in the same binary referenced std_algebra zero times. One binary, one declaration, the type checker honouring it and the evaluator contradicting it. THE RUNG THIS SAT AT WAS NOT A RUNG: the interpreter did not refuse, it answered confidently and wrongly, which DESIGN section 4b places OUTSIDE the ladder. A REPAIR EXISTS AND IS DELIBERATELY UNLANDED (gunbc#10187, DRAFT, not merged): the two arms WOULD build optional_present/optional_absent, mirroring map_lookup_as_optional, which already performs exactly this for maps -- and the reason it is withheld is the PRIOR FILING recorded below, not an unfinished implementation. NO CLIMB IS CLAIMED BY THIS ROW. The class stands where it stood -- 'decided by call site, not value shape, so a stored V = Optional<T> payload is never mistaken for an already-wrapped result' -- prior art in the same file rather than a new mechanism. THE REALIZATION PICTURE IS THE PART FUTURE READERS SHOULD NOT FLATTEN, AND IT HAS THREE COLUMNS RATHER THAN ONE VERDICT. VERIFIED BY EXECUTION: rust emission nests faithfully; and the python and typescript TYPE CONSTRUCTORS cannot carry the nesting at all -- typing.Optional[typing.Optional[int]] EVALUATES TO typing.Optional[int] and compares equal, and in the typescript realization `{0} | undefined` the absent element and the absent selection are the same value (undefined === undefined). No method template can repair that, because the type it would have to return does not exist in those targets; a boxed encoding would be a different realization of Optional, not a better template. REFUTED BY EXECUTION: the inference that go is faithful BECAUSE its optional row is `*{0}` and **T is a legal Go type. The row permits the nesting and the emitter did not do it -- go emitted *int64 for a declared Optional<Optional<Int>>. THE ROW PREDICTS CAPABILITY, NOT BEHAVIOUR, and that is the transferable lesson here: the python collapse and the go faithfulness were derived the same way, from the optional row, and only one survived execution. UNOBSERVED: what python and go emission actually ANSWER end-to-end, because for this input neither artifact runs -- go returns two values from single-return signatures and references an undefined Present, python uses Optional and Present with neither imported nor defined. Those sit in accepted_source_emits_uncompilable_target and are UPSTREAM of this class; the denotation question is not reachable there until the artifacts compile. NO REFUSAL IS FILED FOR THOSE PATHS AND THAT IS DELIBERATE. A refusal on unrepresentable nesting would be a wall in front of a hole, and for typescript it would be unreachable in any case: main.rs parses only rust | python | go | dag and RenderTarget has no TypeScript variant, so no fixture can drive that path and any check over it would be permanently green by construction. Recorded here as an OBSERVED BOUNDARY with its evidence rather than as a check that establishes nothing. PRIOR FILING, FOUND AFTER THE REPAIR WAS WRITTEN AND BEFORE IT LANDED, WHICH IS WHY THE REPAIR IS DEFERRED RATHER THAN SHIPPED: this seam is ALREADY FILED as audit-queue item 0 of docs/plans/compiler-guarantee-recovery-gap-analysis.md (2026-08-27, from gunbc#9491), and extdeps.bmc.pid_control_decode carries the minimal reproduction inline plus an admitted workaround -- it renames its own field to magnitude on exactly this ground and claims no compiler guarantee. Item 0 states the class as TWO questions and warns the eventual owner not to collapse them. (1) MEMBER-SELECTION POLICY: v1.compiler.infer_lookup field_summary_for_type opens `if field == "value" && normed_opt` and unwraps otherwise, so FIELD ACCESS THROUGH AN OPTIONAL AUTO-UNWRAPS FOR EVERY SPELLING EXCEPT `value` -- which item 0 calls a language-semantics decision, not obviously a bug, with three candidate answers. (2) TYPE CONFORMANCE, below floor independently of (1): a record-valued expression accepted where an Int is required. THAT PRIOR FILING RECLASSIFIES THIS ROW'S OWN REPAIR AND THE RECLASSIFICATION IS THE POINT. The type path ALREADY treats first() as optional and silently unwraps field access through it; the evaluator returned a bare element, so that unwrap was a no-op. Making the evaluator construct a real Present{...} therefore does not merely expose consumers that depended on a flattening -- it makes the EVALUATOR INCONSISTENT WITH AN AUTO-UNWRAP THE TYPE CHECKER STILL PERFORMS, in the opposite direction from where the two halves started, which is not a climb. Honouring the declaration at the evaluator requires the matching evaluator-side unwrap, and that IS item 0(1)'s open policy question. SO THE INTERPRETER/EMITTED DIVERGENCE AND ITEM 0(1) ARE THE SAME SEAM APPROACHED FROM TWO SIDES, AND WHOEVER SETTLES THE MEMBER-SELECTION POLICY OWNS THE EVALUATOR REPAIR TOO. Filing them as separate subjects would split one decision across two owners. CORROBORATION FOR ITEM 0(2), independently re-derived here with a control rather than taken from the filing: four arms on a binary BUILT FROM MAIN -- `fn f() -> Bool { 5 }` refuses with 1 error (the positive control, proving the return check is not globally dead), while `fn f() -> Bool { xs().first() }` over a plain List<Int> compiles with ZERO diagnostics. Same result under a binary containing the repair, so the gap is pre-existing and not created by any change here. That is corroboration of a class filed five days ago and is NOT a second finding; a second row for a filed class is the nicknaming refusal at the ledger layer. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, named as a capability: GENERATED ARM BODIES WHOSE RETURN TYPE DERIVES FROM THE ALGEBRA TEMPLATE, so the collapse becomes unwritable rather than merely wrong. Today the arm SELECTION is generated -- v1_interpreter_dispatch_generated.rs -- but from gunbc.v1_interpreter_primitive_surface, a roster that carries no return type and does not import std.algebra. Two rosters name first, neither references the other, and the hand-written body honoured neither; that fork is why the arm and its declared type were separable by hand at all. A POINTER TO ONE SPECIMEN OF THIS SHAPE IN THIS LEDGER'S OWN TOOLING, 2026-09-03: a referential-closure check over these rosters that JOINS BY NAME cannot see two declarations sharing one name whose CONTENT differs. Measured by injection -- main's `recurrence_ledger_scoped_below_the_recurrence` duplicated with a pre-rename spelling of a symbol it cites, 9006 bytes against 8993 -- a name-grain check reports nothing rostered-undeclared and nothing declared-unrostered and PASSES, while the tree carries a citation to a symbol with zero declarations. The guard's state space has no cell for content divergence, so its count is the same either way. What such a check must additionally do is adjudicate any surviving duplicate against main's BYTES and REFUSE when neither copy matches, rather than resolve by position or first-wins, which is silent.
- absorbing fallback (a failure arm that widens — rerun everything, scan all keys, always run — instead of refusing; ⊤-as-answer conflated with ⊤-as-ignorance; the deficit's frequency zeroed by construction, the corpus-denominated cost breaking the budget later)
- **empty-observation narrow** (the absorbing fallback's mirror: ⊥-as-answer conflated with ⊥-as-ignorance — an observation that could not express what changed rendered as the verdict "nothing is affected", so the run narrows to nothing instead of widening. Strictly worse than the widen §5 already forbids: a widen is merely expensive, a narrow is silently uncovered. Live specimen: a push whose baseline ref *is* the pushed ref diffs the commit against itself, and the witness floor reported the resulting zero-path observation as "N unaffected (import-closure, skipped without resolve)" — receipt, two main pushes reporting an identical `4118 unaffected` on different commits, a skip count that cannot be a function of the diff. The tell that one observation is wrong rather than three local choices reasonable: its consumers disagree — compile-clean widened to whole-tree, regen gated on the event name, the witness floor skipped) **RECEIPT, AND IT MOVES THE CLASS FROM AN OBSERVATION'S EXPRESSIVENESS TO AN ANALYSIS'S RANGE** (quick-crab-396, 2026-09-02). Authoring a coproduct arm named `Refused`, I ran a corpus-wide collision census over TYPE names, got a clean answer, and authored the name. required-regen then refused: `variant 'Refused' appears in both 'TypeRealizationDecision' and 'EmitterOutcome'`, and re-running the census over VARIANT spellings found `Refused` live in SIX declaring types. THE DEFECT WAS NOT A MISSING ANALYSIS. The compiler already computes exactly this collision -- it is what refused -- but it computes it during v2 self-compile over ONE RESOLVED CLOSURE, and the question being asked was about the CORPUS. Same subject, two ranges. From the author's seat the narrow analysis's silence was indistinguishable from a negative result, which is this row's own conflation with the scope quantifier moved: not an observation that could not EXPRESS what changed, but one that could not REACH where the answer lived. WHY IT IS FILED HERE RATHER THAN AS A NEW CLASS: the mechanism is identical and only the axis differs, and DESIGN section 2 prices a fresh authority for an existing concept as a failed decomposition. RECOGNITION RULE: when a check answers clean, ask what POPULATION it ranged over and whether that is the population the question was about -- an analysis scoped to the wrong range is, from the caller's seat, indistinguishable from an analysis that does not exist. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER for this instance, named as the capability and not as an artifact: A CORPUS-WIDE NAMESPACE CENSUS OVER EVERY TYPE DECLARATION AND EVERY COPRODUCT ARM, sufficient to answer 'is this spelling already declared anywhere in the corpus' before a name is authored, rather than after emission. ONE census over the one Node tree, not a second check beside the type-name one -- two checks over one namespace is the range split that produced this instance.
- **CLAIM-FORCED AND UNBRACKETED -- a shared memoized host artifact whose identity is not knowable before the fold and which records no fill bracket.** INVALID STATE: the artifact is shared, so a later claim reads it warm, but nothing brackets the fill, so the whole build lands on whichever claim reached it first. HARM, AND THE REASON THIS CLASS HIDES: the number that lands is REAL -- a true measurement of something, charged to a row that does not own it -- SO NOTHING LOOKS WRONG. The row's reported cost becomes a function of DISCOVERY ORDER rather than of the row, and because the required floor's safety limit is a FAIL-STOP, an order-dependent number decides whether the line stops. DISTINGUISHING FACT, AND IT IS THE FORCING CLASS RATHER THAN ANY OBSERVED FIELD: an artifact every identity of which is knowable before the fold is PREPARATION-FORCEABLE -- the floor builds it ahead and bills preparation, no claim window ever contains that build, and it therefore carries NO fill bracket CORRECTLY. ABSENCE OF A BRACKET IS NOT EVIDENCE OF A DEFECT, and reasoning from that absence is the specific misread this class invites. An artifact only a claim can force must record its fill, at which point the claim is charged the marginal half. The third state is the conjunction: claim-forced AND unbracketed. RECOGNITION RULE, AND IT IS NOT AVAILABLE FROM THE COST LOG: a cost log reports what a run OBSERVED, while regime membership is a property of the CONSTRUCTION -- is the artifact memoized, is it shared, is every identity it can be asked for knowable before the fold. Those are decidable by reading the producer and are not recoverable from a measurement, so a row's absence from shared-fill accounting is equally consistent with preparation-forceable, with not-shared-at-all, and with this class -- and the floor output cannot separate the three. SPECIMEN OF THE MISCLASSIFICATION RATHER THAN OF THE CLASS (2026-09-03): one subject was placed in four different regimes in three hours, every placement derived from floor output and each one propagated onward, and it was settled by reading the call site -- v1_interpreter eval_fn_arrow_decl_facts_live has no memo, cache or fill bracket at all, so there is no first toucher and no free later reader, and it reaches the interpreter as a HOST BRIDGE ARM and therefore has no resolved .dag fn node to install in a roster keyed on such nodes. Its sharing tier is unreachable BY CONSTRUCTION rather than by omission, and its cost is its own. THAT ROW IS CORRECTLY BILLED, AND ADMITTING IT TO A MISATTRIBUTION CENSUS WOULD CORRUPT THE POPULATION -- six callers each paying the same unshared walk is a real defect, but it is SHARED WORK NEVER MADE SHAREABLE, whose cost can be made cheap, and not one row carrying cost it does not own. PRIOR INCIDENT, WITH A NUMBER: the census memo sat in this state from gunbc#9428 and refused the required floor at 5812ms against a 5000ms fail-stop while two later claims read the same compiles free. COMPOUNDING RISK, WHICH IS WHY THIS OUTRANKS AN ORDINARY COST DEFECT: gunbc#9517 proposes freezing rows above the line in a shrink-only contract, and a row frozen for cost it does not own can never be made cheap, so it can never leave -- a misattributed measurement becomes PERMANENT. RUNG FOUND AT: mitigatable, on review diligence, which is the authoring change's own honest self-assessment and not a grade assigned here -- the two treatments are two hand-written call sites with no carrier relating them, so this state stays WRITABLE and nothing refuses it. CEILING: STRUCTURALLY IMPOSSIBLE, and it is attainable rather than aspirational because forcing class is DECIDABLE from the artifact's own construction -- the ceiling is not limited by decidability but by a missing carrier. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, QUOTED VERBATIM FROM THE AUTHORING CHANGE RATHER THAN RE-COINED, because re-wording an existing well-formed trigger is the nicknaming DESIGN section 3 forbids: a memoized host artifact DECLARES its forcing class and the warm-or-net treatment is DERIVED from that declaration, at which point the third state has no spelling and the bracket cannot be forgotten. It names a CAPABILITY rather than an artifact, which is what section 4b(3) requires. WHY THIS ROW EXISTS AT ALL, GIVEN THAT THE ANALYSIS ABOVE WAS ALREADY AUTHORED: the rung sentence and its trigger were written on 2026-08-27 in gunbc#9477 as a `//` ANNOTATION in gunbc.shared_fill_attribution_seed_growth, and section 4c is explicit that no Accepted program can read an annotation -- the file says so about itself, that nothing refuses this state and the next memo reintroduces it. The TYPED row in that module is a SeedGrowthJustification whose trigger is to DELETE two hand-Rust functions once the memoized compiles are MODELED operations; that is a different subject and would be discharged by a change leaving this state exactly as writable as it is today. The class appeared in NEITHER ledger section 4b names until this row, which is why filing it forks nothing. RELATION TO THE CAPABILITY ROW, CITED ONE-WAY FROM HERE AND DELIBERATELY NOT BACK, AND WITH AN EXPLICIT LIMIT ON WHAT THE CITATION BUYS -- an earlier draft of this sentence claimed the two triggers CANNOT DRIFT because each row named the other, and that was an overclaim of exactly the kind section 4b(1) forbids: `RecurringFailureMode` and `RungDrop` carry NO TYPED RELATION FIELD, so editing either trigger invalidates nothing, refuses nothing and fails no check. Cross-citation makes the relationship REVIEW-VISIBLE and nothing stronger; a typed parent reference would be a different design and is what a mechanical anti-drift claim would require. THE CITATION IS ONE-WAY BECAUSE THE NARROWER ROW OWNS THE RELATIONSHIP and because the parent declares in its own opening that IT NAMES NO CAUSE -- enumerating mechanisms there would start undoing the rename that removed a refuted attribution from its identity. So: gunbc.rung_drop `floor_cost_claim_qualification_unavailable` declares the GENERAL loss -- claim cost is not a stable property of the claim -- and names its closure-level component insensitive to the claim's own assertion work. THIS ROW IS ONE NAMED MECHANISM DEFEATING THAT INVARIANCE, nested inside it rather than disjoint from it, and it is filed separately because it has its own narrower and INDEPENDENTLY SATISFIABLE trigger: buried inside the capability row, discharging this mechanism would read as discharging the capability, which it does not. Satisfying this trigger removes one mechanism and leaves that row standing on its position-sensitive component, whose cause and bound remain unestablished.
- cache impurity (key on declared-input content; byte-identical cached-vs-cold is the purity oracle)
- reflection evidence ≠ structural proof (prove a read axis by execution, with a no-host-enumeration control)
- coercion proven by normalized round-trip, not a golden string
- parallel-representation debt (an honestly-marked scaffold duplicating a canonical fact is still a violation)
- internal review finds missing tests, external review finds missing checks
- unmarked workaround (an author-side absorbing fallback — routing around an obstacle with no dissolution trigger; the line-stop signal, never a landing state)
- **self-authorized dissolution** (temporary work treated as admissible because the author named its future deletion — a trigger is a lifecycle fact, not permission; without an operator verdict, marked debt is rejected exactly like unmarked debt)
- positional citation (a `file:line` pointer where a symbol was available — see §3). **THE EDIT FORM IS THE SAME CLASS AND IT ALSO WRITES.** An edit anchored on NEIGHBOURING TEXT -- sed on a surrounding line, a string replace keyed on the adjacent entry, an insert before a named sibling -- is a positional handle by another spelling, and it decays for the identical reason: anything that changes above or beside the anchor invalidates it. The difference is the consequence. A stale citation misleads a reader who can still see the text; a stale ANCHOR silently does NOTHING, because a replace that matches zero times reports success. **RECEIPT (sunny-gull-270, 2026-09-02, this carrier).** Appending the row `hedged_benefit_leaves_the_harm_axis_unexamined` needed two edits: the declaration and the roster-list entry. The roster append was anchored on `one_refusal_two_destinations,` followed by the closing bracket. Between reading the file and writing it, main gained `liveness_probe_read_as_currency` as the new last entry, so the anchor stopped matching, the declaration landed and the roster entry did not, and NOTHING FAILED -- the result would have been a declared-but-unrostered class, exactly the half-applied state a roster cannot detect about itself. **WHAT CAUGHT IT IS THE TRANSFERABLE PART, and it is not vigilance: `grep -c` returning 1 where 2 was owed.** The instruction is to CHECK THE RESULT RATHER THAN THE ACTION, because a no-op edit and a successful one are indistinguishable at the actuator -- the same discipline that catches a stale citation by reading `git show origin/main:<path>` rather than the working tree, which a manager on this same exchange used to avoid refuting a correct citation from a pinned checkout. Two instances, one root: a read and a write separated by someone else's push. **RECOGNITION RULE: any edit whose anchor is text the edit does not own. State the expected post-condition as a COUNT before applying it, and assert the count afterwards; if you cannot say what the count should become, the edit is not specified.**
- **bound-shaped closure** (a requirement is satisfied by a number that is real, and the row it satisfies stops ranking for work. Distinct from prefix-of-the-truth, which is about what a PRODUCER reports: this is about what a CONSUMER concludes from a satisfied clause. Receipt (gunbc#9418): §4b(3) asks a declared drop for a bounded population; a whole-root emit census answers three; the emitter's first gate had returned ahead of every later check, and clearing it showed eight more that had been standing the whole time. Publishing three as THE population would have discharged 4b(3) with an honest measurement and left the class reading as closed — worse than the UNCOUNTED it replaced, because an unmet clause still ranks and a met one does not. The tell is that the satisfying figure comes from a mechanism that STOPS: a first match, an early return, a capped scan, a truncated list. **Recognition rule: before a number discharges a requirement, ask what the producer does AFTER it has enough to answer — if it returns, the number bounds the producer's patience and not the population.**)
- **authority substitution** (fact F lives in carrier A; operation O is governed by carrier B; A is cited as the evidence that O changed; no A→B relation is claimed by either carrier. Nothing asserted is false — the *entailment* is invented, which is why review reads past it: both halves check out and only the arrow between them is missing. It is §3's single-authority rule read in the direction authors actually violate it — not two authorities for one fact, but one authority borrowed to answer for another's. Receipt (gunbc#8865): an expecting-red probe was enrolled in `gunbc.explicit_witness_admission`, which DOCUMENTS a known red, and the PR asserted the required floor therefore held it; the floor gates on `v2.workflow.floor_expected_red` and nothing relates the two, so CI went red on a row its author had recorded as expected. The general tell is a sentence of the form *I registered it in A, so B now does X* where B is never named — and its structural fix is never a broader guard joining every A row to B, since that manufactures exactly the entailment being invented; the guard must carry the narrow predicate, with its exceptions declared.)
- **reachability read as occupancy** (a guard's arm, disposition or check is deleted because nothing currently lands in it. Three questions are in play and only the first two decide whether it should exist: *mechanism existence* — can any governing authority produce this state; *join reachability* — can that mechanism classify an element of THIS operation's denominator; *current occupancy* — does anything receive it today. **Yes / yes / zero is a healthy guard being quiet, not a dead one**, and deleting it removes a live wall while looking principled. The distinction is load-bearing because it borders a true rule pointing the other way: a check whose RED IS UNREACHABLE — no authorable input can produce it — is a decoration, permanently green and worse than absent, because it is cited as coverage. Unreachable means the red cannot be produced at all; empty means it can and nothing does. Two corollaries, each from a lane that got it wrong first. Reachability is judged against what a FIXTURE MAY AUTHOR, never what the accepted corpus contains — a regression probe is an invalid program by definition, so a state no `Accepted` program holds is usually still authorable as source, and scoping reachability to corpus-expressible is this same conflation one layer out. And an arm is reachable relative to ONE operation: `gunbc.scm`'s path-exclusion disposition is unreachable from the floor's own fold, which drops excluded subjects before inventory, yet reachable from a join over admission ROWS, which exclusion never removes — so *the arm's reachability is a property of the denominator of the operation that returns it, not of what some lower mechanism observes internally*. Both halves were found the same day by separate lanes, from opposite directions. **THE DISCRIMINATING COUNTER-SPECIMEN, which this row needed and did not have (gunbc XL-0-RECEIPT, 2026-09-01):** `gunbc.floor_component_receipt` and `gunbc.floor_component_receipt_document` were a well-built pair — schema, artifact name, path, a typed subject carrying workflow name / run id / head sha, a decoder with typed `ReceiptMemberAbsent` and `ReceiptMemberMalformed` causes, and an event-subject join refusing over three distinct mismatch arms. Read against the three questions the answer is not yes/yes/zero but **NO at the first**: nothing anywhere writes `target/floor-component-receipt.json`, no Rust carries the schema string, and `witnesses.yml` uploads four artifacts of which the receipt is not one. Their reader was the `falsifier-alert.yml` job, deleted in the 2026-08-15 CI cut. **The join is UNREACHABLE, not merely unoccupied, and that is the whole difference between retiring a dead model and deleting a live guard** — a quiet guard has a producer that could fill it, this had none. The census must be run per medium, since a survivor stops being reached silently: import graph (only its own witness test), `*_test.dag` discovery (that test is a PEER of the subject, not a guard over a survivor), workflow argv, Cargo `[[bin]]`, Rust (one string literal used as a test label, not a call), and roster rows (two `gunbc.non_fold_residue` frontier rows). **The second, costlier half — what the prose did while the model sat dead — is its own class and is rostered separately as `unbacked_execution_claim`;** what belongs HERE is only the consequence for this rule: a dead model is not harmless while it waits, because **a good model with no consumer is still residue (§6), and residue that describes itself in the present tense actively manufactures false premises.** Disposition landed: both modules and their witness test deleted, the two frontier rows removed, and every surviving citation of them rewritten to the past tense with the deletion and its date recorded — because a citation left naming a deleted symbol fakes a grep hit for the next reader.)
- **total at the level examined, blind one level down** (a judgment is exhaustive over the type it matches and says nothing about the distinction that actually decides the outcome, which lives inside one of its arms. **The pairing is the whole content: the match is exhaustive AND wrong**, and the exhaustiveness is what hides it — there is no missing arm to report, no warning, nothing incomplete, so neither a reviewer reading the match nor a wildcard-free lens can see it. Specimen with a mutation receipt (gunbc.scm, 2026-08-22), **repaired on main 2026-08-25 by gunbc#8973 and kept in the past tense because the mechanism recurs while this instance does not**: a publication fold matched one outer arm carrying a JSON codec's entire refusal enum. It was total over that codec's refusal type, and the enum's arms carried three different publication remedies — a reader limitation, an absent-object problem over a well-formed document, and malformed bytes. Only the third may supersede a head slot, so the collapsed form let an OLDER READER OVERWRITE A NEWER WRITER'S DOCUMENT: data loss produced by the reader's own limitation, from a match that compiled and looked complete. Executed both ways — descending, four witnesses passed; collapsed, the witness driving all three remedies through the single outer arm went red; a temporary decode arm made the fold non-exhaustive; restored control passed. **None of this row's three cited symbols resolved on main, and they failed in two different ways** — measured across main's whole history, not just its head. `repository_load_disposition`, the function this row said performed the collapse, has NEVER existed as code on main: it appears only in this carrier and in the projection, and it names a declaration from the CLOSED attempt gunbc#8940, whose branch was retained in history and never landed. So the row cited a function that was fabricated on the day it was written. `RepositoryDecodeImageRefusal` and `ImageRefusal` were real — they landed with gunbc#8807 and gunbc#8840 and were deleted by gunbc#8965 on 2026-08-23 when the fused format was cut — so those two went stale one day later, by the ordinary route a citation rots when the thing it names is removed. The two halves matter separately because they have different remedies: a fabricated name is caught by resolving symbols before writing them, a deleted one only by a resolver that reruns. Either way a reader applying this entry's own recognition rule grepped for a match that was not there. That is §3's stale-citation class sitting inside the authority that names it, and it is recorded here rather than quietly deleted because a specimen whose symbols cannot be resolved is the failure this row exists to teach, one level up. **What landed is stronger than descending**, and the difference is the reusable part: the remedy vocabulary was hoisted into a module that names no format — `gunbc.scm.load_standing` `LoadStanding`, and each format now classifies its own refusals into it (`gunbc.scm.commit_closure_json_v2` `closure_document_load_standing`). Descending inside one codec's enum would have closed the collapse and left the consumer still matching on a format's vocabulary; hoisting leaves the consumer no format arms to collapse, so the next format is a new classifier rather than a new way to reintroduce this defect. **THE HOISTING WAS NECESSARY AND NOT SUFFICIENT, and this sentence is the fourth citation in this row to have gone stale.** It read that the permission was derived `by one total function`, naming `standing_may_supersede_generation` — a `LoadStanding -> Bool` carrying neither the slot the observation was made on nor the generation examined, so a caller could apply it to a slot the evidence never described or to a generation that had moved since it was read. gunbc#9258 deleted it and replaced the destination with `gunbc.scm.supersession` `supersede_head_slot`, where evidence is minted FROM the slot by `observe_generation` — a caller cannot state which generation it examined, only examine one — and the swap refuses unless the slot still holds it. The deletion left this sentence naming a declaration that no longer resolves, one day after it was written, which is the same rot this row already records twice above and is corrected here for the same reason. Second, independent specimen the same day, in the compiler itself: a census of the fourteen declared-type positions the grammar admits found five accepting a plain kernel value where a coproduct is declared — the judgment is present and total at the level it examines, and never descends. **The tell is a `_` or a bare binding in the arm that carries the payload**, and the test is not whether the match is complete but whether every cause it collapses shares one remedy; the fix is to descend so a new inner arm fails to compile HERE, rather than silently inheriting whichever remedy the outer arm happened to name.)
- **execution-provenance loss** (a reported value is consumed as though an observation produced it, while the carrier holds no proof the observation path ran. **Distinct from state-space conflation, and the repair law is what separates them**: there the carrier cannot represent the distinction and the fix is to split the states; here the carrier already has the state — the consumer simply cannot tell whether it was *reached*, and the fix is to bind an execution receipt to the value. Three specimens in one night, all on main, none of them a missing variant. `known_red_held=206` is `36 held + 170 that RUNTIME-ERRORED`: the substrate splits those into distinct arms, so the states exist; what the quoted figure loses is *did the assertion run*, and an expected-red row that threw is not evidence the red is expected — it is a row whose assertion never executed. A floor whose `TerminalLedgerUnrenderable` count is `0` reads identically whether the ledger checks passed or preparation refused before a ledger existed. And a `.dag` board of `E0599=23 E0308=128` reads identically whether cargo ran or normalize refused upstream of it. **The receipt that makes this worth a row rather than a caution:** for three hours after #8976 put a §4c-illegal annotation inside a match body, the compiler refused at strict preparation while downstream measurements kept producing valid-looking values — one lane concluded a defect was closed, another concluded the same defect was still firing, both citing runs where it could not appear at all; three separate A/B comparisons reported perfect agreement, one because both arms were broken identically, one because a missing flag meant no arm executed, and one because the defect had been fixed between runs so both arms were the same bytes. Every one of those is downstream of a single fact: **a masked run and a clean run rendered identically.** Recognition rule: if a count of zero and an unreached stage produce the same output, the number is not an observation. The construction move, and it is stronger than naming known masks because it also forecloses the undiscovered ones: any stage capable of preventing a downstream observation from executing must participate in the observation carrier, so `refused before stage N` cannot inhabit the same carrier as `stage N executed with zero findings` — a known mask is a hole someone found, execution provenance is what stops an unfound hole from masquerading as a measurement.)
- remediation-mutated view (the standard response to a signal OVERWRITES the surface most readers query it through, so the population becomes unreconstructible AT THAT LEVEL and the reader concludes the evidence is gone. It usually is not: the defect is reading a MUTABLE PROJECTION as if it were the record, and a level below it there is generally an immutable one the same query reaches intact. Receipt, and the inversion belongs BESIDE it rather than after it: a fleet event terminated eight witnesses runs inside 49 seconds; every lane re-ran; a re-run REPLAYS the original merge ref and OVERWRITES the run-level conclusion, so queried through run conclusions the population was invisible -- zero of twenty-one -- while queried through the run's attempt records it was complete and exact hours later. Two independent reconstructions from the mutated view reported five and six of the eight: each correct, each incomplete, same cause -- so the mutated view fails SILENTLY and PARTIALLY, which is worse than failing empty. The rule is therefore NOT capture-or-lose-it: capture before remediating, or reconstruct from the immutable record, never from the mutable one. Stating only the first half is worse than saying nothing, because a lane that has already remediated reads it as a verdict that its evidence is gone and stops looking -- which is what produced the zero of twenty-one. Recognition rule: before concluding a population is unrecoverable, ask WHICH LEVEL you queried and whether the remedy WRITES to it.)
- diagnostic name accurate about the situation, silent about the mechanism (a diagnostic's NAME describes the state it was raised in faithfully and says nothing about WHICH PREDICATE produced it, so a reader who treats the name as a cause builds a coherent story with no load-bearing contact with the code. Nothing in the name is wrong, which is why review reads past it. THREE RECEIPTS IN ONE NIGHT FROM THREE READERS, provenance stated per receipt because a row about untraced names must not itself rest on an untraced relay. FIRST, measured by this row's author, and it leads because the reader was the author of the story: rustc labels reading `expected Rc<Vector<i64>>, found String` at 49 sites plus 2 reversed, from which a representation fork was inferred -- the model type `Symbol` rendering as `String` in parameter position and a vector in field position -- and published. Measured: `Symbol` renders CONSISTENTLY as `String`, `Connective.Atom` carries `identity: String`, and the `.dag` is well-typed with the same declared type on both sides. The published mechanism was wrong; the label was a faithful description of a mismatch the type-checker had computed. AND THE CHECK FAILED THE SAME WAY THE READING DID, which is the most instructive part rather than decoration: a grep for the printed type name returned zero and zero was read as absence, when the emitted source spells `Rc<Vec<i64>>` and `lib.rs` carries `use im::Vector as Vec`, so rustc was printing the resolved underlying name. A name was read as a fact about the tree twice in one investigation, once in the diagnostic and once in the refutation of it. WHAT THE 49 SITES ACTUALLY ARE, and it is UNCHASED: `Rc<Vec<i64>>` is how `List<Int>` renders, so they read expected `List<Int>` found `String` -- an ordinary mechanism, and a real one. The amendment closed the wrong explanation, not the defect. SECOND, reported by quick-bat-372 and independently verified: `MethodNotFound` names the situation correctly and does not say which predicate gates it -- `method_existence_decision` gates on `kernel_profile_lookup`, so a fully resolved USER product with no kernel profile answers `MethodExistenceUndecided`. THIRD, same provenance: `FieldNotFound` names a concept never emitted for field ACCESS at all, established by exhausting its three construction sites -- two pattern bindings and one guarded by a record literal's field names. WHY IT EARNS A ROW: two of the three would have shipped as PERMANENTLY-GREEN WITNESS ARMS, an assertion that a wall fired over a wall that cannot fire for that input, which is section 4b's decoration-cited-as-coverage reached by a route no other row names -- and all three were caught by reading the PRODUCER rather than by review. WHAT IS NOT CLAIMED: three instances in one night is a suggestive rate and not a base rate; all three surfaced while several lanes were unusually deep in diagnostic populations, so the frequency is a property of that work rather than of the corpus. Recognition rule: before treating a diagnostic name as a mechanism, read the function that produces it and find the predicate that gates it; if you cannot name that predicate, you have a situation and not a cause. A grep for the type name is a cheap first pass, not a sufficient check: rustc prints resolved names while source spells aliases, so `use X as Y` defeats the search in both directions and a zero can be confident and wrong. Read the producer. AMENDED 2026-08-28 -- THE PRODUCER'S OWN PROSE IS ALSO A NAME, so `read the producer` is necessary and NOT SUFFICIENT. The three receipts above are all DIAGNOSTIC names; the same failure reaches COMMENTS, and it is harder to see there because a comment is where a reader goes to ESCAPE the name. Two variants, and the ranking is the whole point. STALE prose is FALSIFIABLE BY READING: what it says is contradicted by the code as it stands, so a reader who checks the producer can always catch it. THAT IS NOT THE SAME AS SELF-CORRECTING, and an earlier revision of this row said it was -- `it rots when the code moves, and whoever next touches that code notices` -- which THIS ROW'S OWN SECOND RECEIPT REFUTES (review 57329): the deleted/still-here contradiction is LIVE in the tree today, and it was found by a reader who had gone there for an unrelated reason, NOT by the next person to touch the code. So the ranking below is about FALSIFIABILITY and not about correction rate. Stale prose persists exactly as long as nobody reads it, which is indefinitely; what makes it the lesser of the two is that a reader who does check is guaranteed to see the disagreement. WRONG-MECHANISM-CORRECT-CONCLUSION is NOT self-limiting: nothing it says is falsified by moving the code, so it survives every refactor, and the defect it names really existing READS AS CONFIRMATION of the explanation it gives -- load-bearing misinformation with a correct headline, routing every future reader to the wrong repair. RECEIPT, read independently by three readers rather than relayed: the comment above the floor's live-tree scan says its two computations `cannot agree except by coincidence` because one is a syntactic scan and the other falls through to `effect_reach_derived_reads_live_tree_for_entry`, a SEMANTIC reachability derivation -- locating the cause in the import graph. Read the control flow: `reads_live_tree_effective` calls `parse_entry_live_tree_disposition` first, and an UNDECLARED file reaches `Ok(declared.unwrap_or(true))`, so the function RETURNS before the derivation is consulted. The largest and simplest disagreement class is decided before the import graph is reached, and the mechanism the comment names has nothing to do with it. A SECOND RECEIPT FROM THE SAME PARAGRAPH, in the stale variant: it states the floor's copy is DELETED rather than reconciled, in the past tense, four lines above a section headed WHY IT IS STILL HERE explaining that the scan is live. RECOGNITION RULE, extending the one above rather than replacing it: THE CODE PATH IS THE FACT, and every sentence around it -- in a note, in a doc, or in the producer's own comment -- is commentary that may be stale or may be wrong about WHY. And having reached the producer, do not stop at the sentence that confirms your framing: this amendment exists because a reader quoted IT IS NO WALL from such a comment and dropped `and it is a partial classifier, not an enforcement` from four lines below, inside the same citation.)
- identity-absent graph traversal (a walk over a graph the COMPILER built re-enters shared nodes because it cannot carry a `visited` or memo key — the nodes have no identity the model is allowed to name — so the only two arms available locally are to PAY the re-entry, or to key on a substitute the substrate forbids. THE TRAP IS THAT THE ARMS DIFFER IN KIND AND THE CHEAP-LOOKING ONE IS THE UNSAFE ONE: paying is a cost, visible and bounded; keying on a substitute silently skips a DISTINCT node believed already visited, drops whatever that node contributed, and reports nothing. THE MECHANISM, structural and checkable by reading rather than by measurement: `v1.compiler.infer_emit_info` `collect_type_node_import_surface_names` recurses on BOTH `children` AND the `inferred` resolved node, so a shared type DAG is traversed as a tree and a node reachable by k paths is walked k times. The key does not exist. `std.occurrence_identity` `occurrence_identity_scope_law` names filename, SourceSpan, authored name, structural Node equality and content hash as FORBIDDEN identity inputs, and `NodeOccurrenceIdentity` `OccurrenceSynthetic` — which is what the compiler mints for a node no author wrote — carries no id at all, so every synthesised node compares equal to every other and a visited set built on it would skip the wrong one. The remaining candidate is host allocation identity, which exists only in the realization and not in the model. RUNG: OUTSIDE THE LADDER, and the earlier revision of this row said `mitigatable`, which was inflation of exactly the kind section 4b(1) names. The invalid state is writable, nothing refuses it, and its failure is a silent skip — section 4b puts silent wrongness outside the ladder rather than low on it. What that revision offered as the mitigation was that no author has yet needed such a traversal and that review might catch one; neither is containment by a total operation, a typed outcome, a bound, a rollback or an isolation, and no discriminating RED exists on any acceptance path, so no rung above the floor is claimable by executed evidence. ONE PARTIAL CONTAINMENT IS REAL AND DOES NOT LIFT THE CLASS: at the measured seam a dropped name reaches an emitted Rust crate, where rustc refuses it as E0422/E0425/E0433 far from the cause, and the regen byte-identity gate would show the drift. That is a property of THAT seam's consumer being a compiled artifact, not of the class — a traversal whose consumer is a verdict rather than a compilation has no such backstop — and section 4b sets a class's rung at the MINIMUM across its in-scope paths. CEILING: structural impossibility, and per section 4b(3) the trigger names the CAPABILITY rather than an artifact — an identity for synthesised occurrences, sufficient for a `visited` set over inferred type nodes to be authorable in `.dag` at all. Nothing less retires it: a faster traversal, a memo at one seam, or a counter measuring the amplification all leave the key absent and the unsafe arm equally reachable. An intermediate rung is available and is NOT claimed here because nothing implements it: a typed refusal at the traversal seam would make the class mechanically preventable while leaving the invalid state writable. THE ESCAPES THAT LOOK AVAILABLE AND ARE NOT, listed because this class is mostly discovered by trying them, and each was closed by EXECUTION rather than by argument — deleting the producer, replacing it with the cheaper sub-producers beside it, reading `TypeSummary.field_import_surface_names` instead (which is CIRCULAR, being produced BY this walk in `build_field_type_map`, and answers a different relation), and hoisting the work earlier (there is nothing to hoist: each item belongs to one module and each module is emitted once). Two further hypotheses about the COST were falsified the same way and are worth not repeating: the list-copy lowering, and the match-scrutinee deep clone. The measured populations, the amplification, the per-escape figures and the two falsifications are recorded once, dated, with their method and their non-enrollment stated, in the plan document `emit-type-surface-traversal-cost`, which is bound to this traversal and its two consumers in `gunbc.doc_graph_roots` and retires when they move; they are deliberately not transcribed here, because a number copied into a roster row is the positional-citation failure one entry up wearing different clothes. RECOGNITION RULE: when you reach for a visited or memo key over a graph the compiler built rather than the author wrote, enumerate the keys available to you; if every candidate is one the identity authority forbids, the deficit is upstream in the identity algebra and NEITHER local arm is a repair — the amplification is the symptom and the missing identity is the class.)
- generated binding shadows a bare render (an emitter that renders a name by SPELLING while a different generator in the same emission adds a binding for that spelling — a use line, an alias, a prelude — composes into a silent rebinding: each generator is locally correct and the artifact realizes the wrong declaration, with the diagnostic landing at the CONSUMER of the emitted file, far from either producer. SPECIMEN (XL-0T, PR #9720): modules carrying kernel-String data rows plus transitively referenced structural v2.std.text signatures got a generated v2_std_text String use line; the six type renderers' is_host_text_carrier_type short-circuit rendered those kernel rows as the bare spelling String, which the use line then bound to the structural carrier — 160 E0308 rows in one closure, none of them at a line either generator wrote. FIX SHAPE, and the reason this is a class rather than a bug: render by DECLARATION IDENTITY, and give the non-local realization the GROUNDING spelling no local binding can shadow (std::string::String), keeping the bare reference spelling only for the declaration the module's own binding names — the same two-spelling split std.coercion TypeCheckpoint already carries as reference_base vs grounding_base. RECOGNITION RULE: whenever one generator emits a binding for a spelling and any other render site emits that spelling bare, ask which declaration each intends; if they can differ, the bare render needs the grounding spelling or the identity key, because 'correct at every generator' does not compose to 'correct in the artifact'.)
- resolved reference outside the execution closure (an Accepted program contains a resolved call edge whose target declaration's OWNER MODULE is absent from the module closure loaded for that execution, so resolve and typecheck both report success and the program dies at run time.
