MECHANISM: bare-reference resolution consults a declaration population BROADER than the explicit-import closure from which the runtime index is built, so the two phases answer to two different denominators and nothing joins them. THE SPECIMEN, found by execution rather than by reading, and recorded here because a class whose only evidence is an argument is not a class: three witness files -- test.claim.spark_serving_observe_witness_test, test.claim.spark_serving_model_store_witness_test and test.claim.spark.spark_cell_role_retirement_witness_test -- CALLED hermetic_fixture_provenance with no import of the module declaring it. Resolve accepted them, typecheck accepted them, and every claim reaching the call refused at run time with `no declaration named hermetic_fixture_provenance in this execution's loaded index (searched the modules loaded for this run; the name may be declared in a module this run did not load)`. The positive control is exact and cheap: adding the one import admits and executes the UNCHANGED call. WHY IT IS NOT ONE OF THE ROWS BESIDE IT, checked rather than assumed. It is not `reachability_read_as_occupancy`: nothing here mistook reachability for occupancy, and the declaration is genuinely reachable AND genuinely resolved. It is not `identity_absent_graph_traversal`: the declaration exists, has an identity, and that identity is found -- what is absent is the RELATION from the resolved target to the modules the execution loader materializes. This is the missing agreement between two denominators, not the occupancy of either one. RUNG, SCOPED AND NOT INFLATED: mitigatable at the source-to-witness-execution grain the specimen demonstrates, where the runtime refusal is typed, located and loud. THE LANGUAGE-WIDE RUNG IS NOT CLAIMED and the reason is a control that has not been run: place an observable EFFECT before the unloaded call and see whether it executes before the refusal. If it does, the class is not mitigatable at that grain without preflight or rollback, because the failure arrives after the program has already acted. Until that control answers, this row's rung is a statement about witness execution and nothing wider. CEILING: structurally guaranteed rather than structurally impossible -- source can still omit an import, so the state stays writable, but no Accepted program should contain the edge. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, naming the CAPABILITY: acceptance joins every resolved declaration identity's owner module to the exact local/import/prelude closure used to construct the execution index. DISCRIMINATING RED: a bare call with no explicit import is refused BEFORE Accepted, not after. POSITIVE CONTROL: adding the exact import admits and executes the unchanged call. REPAIR DIRECTION, and the wrong arm is the tempting one: constrain RESOLUTION and ADMISSION. Do NOT widen the runtime loader to chase bare-reference edges -- that converts undeclared dependencies into implicit imports and dissolves the direct-import law to make a symptom go away, which is the absorbing-fallback shape applied to a language rule. Both phases must consume ONE admitted dependency closure. RECOGNITION RULE: a green typecheck is not evidence that a call can execute unless the phase that accepted it and the phase that will run it were answering to the same module set; when a name resolves but its module is not imported, ask which denominator each phase used before concluding the reference is sound.)
- **surface shorthand preempts resolved identity** (a semantic consumer dispatches on a raw lexeme after resolution has already named its declaration, so a declared constructor is reinterpreted as a host sentinel or primitive. Recognition rule: once a node carries a binding/declaration identity, value construction must be total over that identity; spelling may be interpreted only on an explicitly unresolved shorthand arm. Specimen gunbc#9741: bare and imported `None` became host `Null` while qualified `Diagnostics.None {}` became a `Variant`; a single-frame comparison hid the fork. This is not hollow alias: the binding is grounded and present, then discarded downstream. It is also separate from the per-frame symbol-ordinal defect: that defect corrupted lexeme identity while crossing frames, whereas this one ignores an identity already supplied at one construction site. Next-rung trigger: host literals and intentional primitive representations are typed parser/resolver constructions, and evaluator value construction is total over every resolved binding with no raw-spelling arm able to override it.)
- **meaning fork** (one scoped name carries more than one material semantic referent under hidden state; accuracy of meaning applies to terms in every layer, and for a contract-bearing name, differences in obligations, quality floor, refusal behavior, billing consequence, or remedy establish different referents. The subject is scoped: the key is the naming surface, the visible name, and the declared effective version or epoch — the same spelling in two explicitly distinct scopes or across a declared version transition is legitimate reuse, not a fork. Recognition rule: hold that key constant and vary the hidden state; if the material semantic referent changes without a distinct variant name, the name has forked — for a contract-bearing name, a change in obligations, quality floor, refusal behavior, billing consequence, or remedy is sufficient evidence of that change. This is the one-name/two-meanings dual of nicknaming.)
- **externalized degradation** (an actor that accepted an obligation or risk, or caused a cost, silently shifts the resulting burden to another principal while preserving the apparent name, price, or contract. Recognition rule: identify the accepted obligation or causal responsibility, trigger the adverse state, and follow who bears the marginal burden; if another principal's burden rises while the responsible actor neither absorbs it through its reserve, refusal, or remedy path nor exposes a separately named, priced, and refusable transfer, the burden was externalized. A disclosed, refusable allocation agreed before contract formation is the transfer arm; an undisclosed or unilateral later shift is refused. The honest arms are absorb-and-reserve or separately name-and-price the transfer.)
- mistyped body radiates nonlocal phantom diagnostics (LAYER, stated first because carrying this finding across it inverts its meaning: the producer is the .dag COMPILER diagnosing .dag SOURCE -- the interpreter/type-check path behind gunbc compile and the --required-regen self-compile -- NOT rustc diagnosing emitted Rust; nothing here supports discounting any rustc-diagnostic census. THE FAILURE: a type error confined to ONE module-scope fn body does not refuse at that body with a located diagnostic; it radiates as a fabricated population of field-access diagnostics at UNRELATED modules, so the line stops pointing at the wrong factories. SPECIMEN (XL-0T, PR #9720): one fn added to v1.compiler.infer_env whose body held NonEmptyStr-as-String casts in comparison/argument position plus a FilePath-vs-String if-arm mismatch produced ~700 advisory `no field 'source_indices' on type 'TypeEnv'` rows across compile.dag, 05_emit_rust.dag and 04_resolve.dag -- modules that were fine -- and blocked required-regen, which printed 12 of them and never named the defective body. A reader begins repairing the wrong files; that misdirection, not the volume, is the harm (section 5: every refusal typed AND located). FOUR-POINT DISCRIMINATOR, one instrument (entry-scoped compile of src/v1/compile.dag, counting the diagnostic): full mistyped body 774; same signature with trivial body 0; casts hoisted into let bindings with structure kept 0 phantoms plus ONE real correctly-located error at the fn's own line; that real error fixed rc=0. The series also refuted the name-capture hypothesis for this specimen, since the fn name was held constant across all four points. RUNG: outside the ladder at the diagnosed-population grain -- the fabricated rows ARE silent wrongness about the modules they name -- while the run-level verdict stayed mitigatable (the compile did refuse; it refused about the wrong subject). CEILING: structurally guaranteed -- an expression-position type failure inside a body refuses AT that body before any cross-module field-access checking consumes the poisoned inference state. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, naming the capability: body-local type-error containment, sufficient that no diagnostic about module B can be produced by a type defect whose every construction site is inside module A. RECOGNITION RULE: when N same-shaped field/type diagnostics appear at unrelated sites immediately after adding or editing ONE declaration, suspect that declaration's body typing before believing any named site; trivial-body the new declaration as the two-minute discriminator, and hoist casts out of comparison/argument position into let bindings to recover the located refusal.)
- **instrument output read as subject content** (a consumer derives a SET from a report whose completeness is a property of the REPORTING TOOL rather than of the subject. **The boundary against `execution_provenance_loss` is the whole justification for a separate row, because the two look identical at the call site and have opposite repairs**: there a value is consumed as though an observation produced it while nothing proves the observation path RAN, and the fix is to bind an execution receipt; here the observation ran and reported FAITHFULLY — the instrument answered its own question correctly — and what is wrong is that its output was complete for the REPORTER s purpose and was read as complete for the CONSUMER s. Provenance is intact; the SUBJECT is substituted, so no receipt binding can detect it and the repair is instead to derive the set from the subject itself. Distinct from `total_at_the_level_examined_blind`, which is a judgment shape (a match total over the type it examines, blind inside an arm), and from `reachability_read_as_occupancy`, which asks whether a quiet guard should exist; this is neither a match nor a guard. Specimen with a receipt, repaired on main by gunbc#9829: the required floor asked `extdeps.git` `git.Core.DiffUnified0` (`git diff -U0 <range>`) which lines a change touched, and `floor_diff_edits_from_line_ranges` attributed those lines to enclosing declarations to decide which witness identities the change established. Git prints only the hunks that DIFFER, so on a rename it prints the module line and whatever else moved — while every identity at the destination is a newly qualified `module.function` that has never executed under that spelling, because the authored module name moved with the file. **THE REASON IT SURVIVED IS THAT THE TWO ANSWERS COINCIDE BY ACCIDENT IN THE COMMON CASE**: for a wholly-added file every line is a `+` line, so line attribution reaches every declaration and the defect is invisible; it appears only when the instrument gets TERSE, which rename detection is. Measured over the window reachable in a shallow session clone — a floor on the population, not the population, since older renames are unmeasured — 8 of 8 rename-destination `.dag` files carrying test decls were under-enrolled and at least 87 of their 103 identities were never selected; a PURE MOVE with no content edit enrolled NOTHING. It was not merely a reporting gap: changed-witness membership is what OVERRIDES the cost-debt withhold and the outside-gate suppression, so the same miss on a rostered identity is a silent decline of a witness whose author is present, which is the state `v2.workflow.floor_changed_witness` `changed_witness_execution_standing` exists to red. The repair derives the set from the subject: a path `parse_unified_diff_added_paths` already rules new-at-path contributes the declarations THE FILE ITSELF PARSES TO, bounded to that file s own decl list and never the corpus, so it is the precise answer rather than an absorbing widen. Its discriminating red is `rename_destination_enrolls_every_test_decl_not_only_the_diff_touched_one`, which feeds the specimen s verbatim diff and FAILS when the rule is disabled, paired with `in_place_modify_enrolls_only_the_touched_test_decl`, which passes under both states so the first cannot be satisfied by widening. **Recognition rule, and it predicts well beyond git**: whenever a set is read out of a tool s output, ask whether that tool promised completeness over YOUR question or over its own — `--stat`, any de-duplicating or truncating reporter, and any porcelain that elides for human brevity all report faithfully and none of them promise the consumer s set. **Its near twin is `executed_conjunct_discriminates_nothing`, approached from the other side**: there the satisfied signal is EXECUTION -- the conjunct genuinely runs and is genuinely gated on -- while here it is COMPLETENESS RELATIVE TO THE REPORTER, which is why this class is not a reachability problem and looking for an unexecuted path finds everything healthy. In both, the axis the diligent reader checks already reads green, and checking it harder returns the same green.)**THREE RECEIPTS FROM ONE SESSION (2026-09-01, XL-0T), ALL READ AS A GREEN OR A ZERO, AND THE THIRD IS THE ONE THIS ROW DID NOT ALREADY COVER.** (1) A remote build read as `ctrl-build --remote -- cargo check ... | tail -8`: the pipeline exits with TAIL's status, so it is zero whatever the compiler said, and the eight-line window it keeps is executor cleanup chatter carrying no line capable of holding an error. Absence of an error read as success AFTER every line that could contradict it had been discarded. (2) A regeneration wrapped in `/usr/bin/time -v` in a container where that binary does not exist: bash died at the wrapper, exited ZERO, the program never ran, and the subsequent `git diff` was EMPTY -- which reads exactly as `no drift`, the answer that would have RETRACTED a correct finding already registered as a prediction. (3) The floor's `[over-cost]` listing, from which order statistics were computed and reported as a distribution -- `median 390ms, p95 418ms, a dense band at 78-84% of the 500ms ceiling`. The listing is not the population and SAYS SO IN ITS OWN LAST LINE: `and 260 further row(s) over the 100ms line, not printed ... this list is the 25 most expensive, ranked on the declared cost basis`, against `over_cost_line_diagnostic=285`. The true shape was the OPPOSITE: one outlier 73ms clear of the field (501 refused, runner-up 428, the remaining 260 all at or below 337ms), and the gap claimed to be empty had a row sitting in it. **WHY THE THIRD IS THE SEVERE ONE AND WHY IT NEEDED THIS ROW RATHER THAN A NEW ONE.** The first two were filters the CONSUMER wrote, and the standing guard -- select on the result pattern, never on position -- catches both. The third was truncation the PRODUCER performed, for its own readability, and DECLARED IN BAND; there was no consumer-authored `head -N` to notice, so the guard does not fire and diligence along it returns green. That is this class exactly: the report was complete for the REPORTER's purpose and read as complete for the CONSUMER's, with provenance intact and the subject substituted. **GENERAL FORM, which extends the recognition rule above from completeness to ORDER: a ranked list is a VIEW, and a view's order statistics are not the population's.** A median or a percentile computed over a top-K is an order statistic of a truncated tail; it carries no trace of the truncation, it has the same shape and units as the real thing, and it is therefore strictly more dangerous than a wrong count, which at least invites the question `out of how many`. So: before computing ANY statistic over a tool's lines, find the producer's own statement of what it printed -- a `N further rows`, a `top K`, a `ranked by`, a total beside the listing -- and where the producer names a fuller artifact, THAT is the population and the log is a preview. **THE HARM WAS NOT TO THE AUTHOR'S OWN CONCLUSIONS**, which is what separates this receipt from the two beside it: the wrong distribution had already been relayed to a lane authoring a DECLARED RUNG DROP whose population is stated as a function of headroom against observed inflation. At the measured 8-11% a row tips only from a baseline near 450ms, so the true at-risk population was ONE row -- the 501 itself -- and the relayed number would have made it twenty-five, inside the one artifact whose entire value is that its population is honest, and in the direction that makes a class look larger, which is the direction nobody audits.)**FOURTH RECEIPT, AND IT WAS FOUND INSIDE THE INSTRUMENT BEING USED TO WRITE THE REPORT ABOUT THIS CLASS (2026-09-01, same session).** `v1.tests.claim.carrier_realization_census`, run over 1320 `.dag` files, prints `subjects=1320` -- and 1320 is the count of FILES INGESTED, not modules CENSUSED. Only 845 modules produce rows; 475, or 36 percent, are silently omitted. The omissions are not obscure: `v2.compiler.parse` is absent, and `v2.std.compilers.lexing` is absent while its siblings `body_lowering`, `cli_surface`, `sugar` and `semantic_decl_emission` are all present, so it is neither a test-module filter nor obvious unreachability. A consumer reads `subjects=` as coverage, which is this class exactly: the count is complete for the REPORTER's purpose -- files it accepted -- and was read as complete for the CONSUMER's, which is modules it examined. **THE SELECTION RULE IS DELIBERATELY NOT STATED HERE**: it was not determined, and guessing it would put a fabricated mechanism in a ledger row, which is worse than an incomplete one. **WHAT IT COST, MEASURED RATHER THAN IMAGINED.** A fixture authored specifically to settle an unrelated question landed in the omitted 475 and produced ZERO rows -- including its own positive control. Its emptiness was exactly what the hypothesis under test predicted, so without the control it would have been reported as confirmation of a hypothesis on evidence containing none. The absence-versus-zero distinction this class names reproduced itself INSIDE the test built to settle it, which is the practical lesson: catching a pattern does not inoculate you against it, and the exposure is highest immediately after being careful. **THE RESOLUTION-AXIS FORM, MEASURED 2026-09-02 (bold-stag-665 specimen i, bold-carp-449 specimen ii): THE INSTRUMENT RESOLVED THE REFERENCE TO SOMETHING ADJACENT TO THE INTENDED SUBJECT AND REPORTED FAITHFULLY ABOUT WHAT IT RESOLVED TO.** The adjacency is a different VERSION or a different SPELLING; and NEITHER specimen here is temporal, which is the clearest statement of where the boundary against `stable_citation_mutable_referent` falls: the time axis went there with the attempt case. Specimen (i) reads one fixed branch against one fixed main, specimen (ii) one fixed tree against one fixed staged copy at one instant; both would answer identically tomorrow, and their adjacency is ANCESTRY and NAMING rather than time.** The forms above substitute WHAT the instrument covered for what the subject contains. Here the instrument resolves to a DIFFERENT VERSION or a DIFFERENT NAME for the same subject, and its answer carries no mark of having done so. **THE TEETH: EVERY RUNG RETURNS A WELL-FORMED PLAUSIBLE ANSWER RATHER THAN AN ERROR, SO THE WRONG ANSWER DOES NOT LOOK LIKE A FAILURE, IT LOOKS LIKE DATA.** `+76/-31` reads as a change; `five functions missing` reads as a deletion. Each arrives with a mechanism ready to explain it, which is what makes it persuasive rather than merely wrong. **THE LADDER, WHICH IS THE TRANSFERABLE PART -- EVERY RUNG BUT THE LAST IS POSITIONAL, AND EACH GOES RED FOR A DIFFERENT IRRELEVANT REASON:** line-diff is red on a REWRITE; identifier-diff is red on a RENAME; concept-mapping is the only test that decides. Identifier-absence is not the cure for line-absence, it is the SAME positional test one level up -- **absence of a NAME is not absence of a CAPABILITY, and only the second is a loss.** **SPECIMEN (i), the line rung -- an ancestry the merge destroyed.** Under SQUASH merge the squashed commit is not an ancestor of the branch, so `git diff --stat origin/main...origin/session/<branch>` re-renders ALREADY-LANDED work as unlanded: gunbc#10100 rendered 3 files, +76/-31, for content wholly present on main. The deciding test is content -- `git merge-tree --write-tree origin/main <branch>` against `git rev-parse origin/main^{tree}`, identical shas meaning the merge is a no-op. A peer hit the same rendering on gunbc#9995 and reported a no-op as a 5313-line partial revert. **SPECIMEN (ii), the identifier rung -- a rename read as a deletion (bold-carp-449, whose session is archived, so this row is the specimen's only durable form).** A worktree held staged residue: `prose_citation_census.dag` staged at 576 lines against main's 802, with 56 lines staged-only; its witness test 245 against 461, 23 staged-only. The first read reasoned from SIZE to CONTENT -- main is 226 lines further along, so these must be earlier phrasings -- which is unsound because a file can grow 226 lines while independently dropping 56. The token test that replaced it pulled distinctive identifiers from each staged-only line and grepped main: 25 of 56 and 15 of 23 were ABSENT, INCLUDING FIVE WHOLE FUNCTIONS AND FOUR WHOLE TEST FNS, which reads as a substantial deletion. IT WAS ENTIRELY RENAMES. Every construct exists in main under different wording -- staged `cross_file: Bool` became main's `ProseCitationLocality = InRetiringFile | InAnotherFile | RetiringFileAmbiguous`; `prose_citation_baseline_row` became `prose_citation_baseline_reading`; `a_citation_in_the_retiring_file_is_not_cross_file` became `a_citation_in_the_retiring_file_is_local_to_it`. **TWO WERE STRICTLY STRONGER IN MAIN** -- the `Bool` became a three-arm coproduct expressing the several-paths case a `Bool` cannot, and main carried an entire second axis the staged copy lacked -- so the instrument reported a LOSS where the truth was a GAIN. What settled it was reading main's type and function definitions and asking which CONCEPT each staged construct maps onto. **RECOGNITION RULE, COVERING BOTH, AND IT MUST FIRE AT READING TIME RATHER THAN AT RETRACTION TIME:** before reading an ABSENCE or a DIFFERENCE as a fact about the subject, establish that the instrument resolved to the subject you meant -- the same version, the same concept -- because every cheap test available resolves to something adjacent and says nothing about having done so. **THE NEAR-MISSES ARE PART OF THE RECEIPT AND ARE WHY THIS IS FILED AT ALL.** Neither reached a wrong decision and each halted one step short: the no-op diffstat was settled by tree sha before anyone acted on reopening; the deletion was settled by concept-mapping before anything was restored. A class whose specimens each halt one step from harm names exactly what the recognition rule has to beat. **BOUNDED AGAINST THE FOUR NEAREST ROWS, and these boundaries are the justification for a form here rather than a fourth authority.** `receipt_names_a_property_not_the_tree_it_holds_of` is a RECEIPT that was valid and EXPIRED when its base moved, repaired by re-deriving against the new base; here nothing expires -- every reading is live and true of its own subject, and the defect is manufactured by JOINING two of them. `positional_citation` is an authored HANDLE that decays and fails by matching zero; here the handle keeps matching perfectly and returns a confident answer about something else -- and note the sharper relation, that specimen (ii) shows an identifier GREP is itself a positional test, so `positional_citation` names the decaying-pointer half while this row names the unsound-absence half. `execution_provenance_loss` asks whether the observation ran; here it ran and reported faithfully. `stable_citation_mutable_referent` is the boundary that decides what this form does NOT carry, and it is the sharpest of the four because the two look identical at the call site -- both are a confident well-formed answer about a subject the reader did not mean. **THE TEST THAT SEPARATES THEM IS NOT WHETHER THEY FEEL DIFFERENT BUT WHETHER THE REPAIRS TRANSFER: does a reader who correctly diagnoses one perform the RIGHT FIX for the other? No -- and each fix is actively wrong applied to the other, which is why this is a boundary rather than a framing.** There the identifier resolves to its own correct subject and the WORLD replaces the content served underneath it, so the reader's test was right and re-reading CONFIRMS the wrong answer; the repair is to pin the citation to something that cannot be replaced -- an attempt number, a sha, an immutable artifact. Here nothing is replaced and nothing moves: every reading is live and would be identical tomorrow, the reader's TEST is what resolved to the neighbouring question, and the repair is to change the test rather than the citation -- line-diff to identifier-diff to concept-mapping. Crossed over, both fixes fail: PIN a squash-merge two-dot and you have pinned a merge base that was always the wrong one, and CONCEPT-MAP two attempts and you conclude a migration is real without ever establishing the two readings share a subject. That is why the ATTEMPT axis is not in the list above and why the job-id receipt is filed there rather than here: an attempt genuinely was replaced, which is that class and not this one. All of them share provenance-intact subject substitution, which is why this is a FORM of this row and not a new one.) **RECEIPT (1) IS NOW MEASURED RATHER THAN ASSERTED, AND A NAMED TOOL IS CLEARED BY THE SAME CONTROL (2026-09-03, vivid-ibex-751).** Receipt (1) above states that a `| tail` pipeline exits with tail's status; it was true and it was prose. The discriminating pair, one line apart on one command: `ctrl-build --local -- cargo check -Z definitely-not-a-real-flag 2>&1 | tail -2` reports rc=0 with PIPESTATUS[0]=101, and the same command unpiped reports rc=101. A positive control and a red on a fixed subject, so the mechanism is now established by execution rather than by description. THE EXONERATION IS THE PART THIS ROW DID NOT CARRY. On the strength of the piped reading, `ctrl-build` was reported to a manager as a fleet-wide false-green generator -- a build wrapper whose exit status did not reflect its compile -- and was one message from being filed as a defect against a shared tool. THE UNPIPED CONTROL CLEARS IT: the wrapper reports the compile's status correctly and always did. The 0 was tail succeeding at printing the failure. That is the first receipt here where a named component was ACCUSED and then CLEARED, and it is recorded beside the accusation deliberately: fabricated debt against a blameless component costs more than the misreading that produced it, because it sends every other lane hunting in the wrong place, and a clearing that lives only in a chat thread does not reach them. The three remedies are not interchangeable: `set -o pipefail` and reading PIPESTATUS[0] both work and both require the author to remember at the exact moment they are not thinking about it; NOT PIPING THE THING WHOSE STATUS YOU NEED is the only unconditionally safe one. AND THE MISATTRIBUTION SURVIVED A WRITTEN NOTE OF THIS EXACT FACT. What stopped it was being asked to file the row: a failure-mode row must name its DISTINGUISHING FACT, and that is the one field that cannot be filled without measuring. **FIFTH RECEIPT, AND IT IS THE SECOND HIT ON THE SAME ARTIFACT (2026-09-03, calm-boar-314).** The required-floor log prints, for a witness interrupted by the cpu deadline, both `cpu_at_least=503ms/500ms` and the sentence `interrupt_point=503ms is where the poll observed the ceiling; every interrupted row reports approximately whatever ceiling interrupted it, so that figure is a property of the BUDGET and not of this row -- do not compare it with the budget or with another row's`, together with `ABOVE 500ms with NO UPPER BOUND (a bound, not a near miss: it is wrong as a cost by an unbounded amount, always low)`. A 926KB log was downloaded, grepped for the blocking populations, and three figures transcribed into margins of 0.6, 0.8 and 5.4 percent against a 500ms ceiling -- percentages computed against a sentence forbidding that comparison, printed on the same line as the figures. Only one of the three was a margin at all; the other two were interrupt points, and a cost unbounded above is INDISTINGUISHABLE THROUGH THIS INSTRUMENT from the step change the reading was offered to rule out, so the conclusion was not merely unsupported but unfalsifiable from the evidence held. **What makes it the second hit rather than a first: the same log's `[over-cost]` listing had already produced the truncated-order-statistics receipt above, so this artifact had already taught this lesson once, to the same reader, on the same file.** The structural argument the margins were decorating -- that a prose string in a ledger row has no path to the CPU cost of an emit test -- was sound alone, which is the harm: the numbers added the appearance of a measured claim to an argument that was already better without them. **WHAT THE TWO 2026-09-03 RECEIPTS SAY TOGETHER, co-signed by both lanes and stated in the form that survives the difference between them.** Two lanes, one hour, unrelated subjects, both reading a STATUS or a FIGURE as a fact about a subject the instrument was not reporting on -- and in the one case where a guard rail WAS printed in band, it did not hold. The narrowing is deliberate and was calm-boar-314's: the strong form -- that both read past an in-band guard rail -- is true of the margin receipt, whose forbidding sentence sat on the line the figures were extracted from, and FALSE of the status receipt, where the `tail` window had already DISCARDED every contradicting line. Nothing was there to read past; the guard rail was absent, not ignored. This row already keeps those apart as consumer-authored versus producer-declared truncation, and the co-signed claim is written so it does not need the weaker case to be stronger than it was. **SECOND SPECIMEN, AND IT MOVES THE REPORTER PROPERTY FROM SCOPE TO VINTAGE** (eager-pike-541, 2026-09-03). The instrument was the locally built `claim_executor`; it ran and answered its own question correctly, and its question was asked under the semantics of an OLDER corpus than the tree under test. After merging a main that had added new compiler seed files, the floor refused with `function parsed_import_statements not found in scope` before any claim executed and reported zero passing claims, whose surface reading is that the branch broke the corpus. It had not; the binary predated the function. THE COMPLETENESS AXIS IS THE SAME AND THE REPORTER PROPERTY IS DIFFERENT -- above, the report was complete for the reporter's SCOPE rather than the subject's; here it was correct for the reporter's VERSION rather than the subject's, which is why this is a specimen of this row rather than a new one. **THE RECEIPT IS THE DESTRUCTIVE ARM**: the same stale binary, asked to regenerate projections, DELETED three live rows from DESIGN.md -- a merged rung drop and two failure modes belonging to other lanes -- while the .dag authorities still declared all three, because a projection generated by an instrument that cannot see a row renders its absence as authoritative. THE GENERATED-ARTIFACT GATE CANNOT CATCH THIS: it compares the projection to its authority and reports clean once both sides agree on the loss, so only the instrument's vintage separates a projection from a deletion. The operative rule is that a local refusal measured against a MOVED main is not evidence about the branch until the instrument is rebuilt against that main.**THE NEXT-RUNG FORMULATION FOR THE RESOLUTION AXIS, AND THE ROW THAT DISSOLVED INTO IT (2026-09-03).** This form was drafted as a separate class, `contingent_instrument_coincidence_read_as_equivalence`, and filed as its own row before the repair-crossing test above was run against it. IT DOES NOT SURVIVE THAT TEST, and running it deliberately rather than arguing the names is what decided this, so the reasoning is recorded here for the next author who arrives with the same shape. THE PROPOSED CLASS: a standing evidence binding from a PROXY INSTRUMENT P to a TARGET QUESTION Q, holding only inside an UNOWNED SUBJECT CONDITION C, where inside C the answers agree and outside it they diverge with the instrument's output identical either way. CROSSING ONE, does this row's repair close that class: applying ESTABLISH THAT THE INSTRUMENT RESOLVED TO THE SUBJECT YOU MEANT to a two-dot comparison means checking whether the base you diff from IS the merge base, which is checking C exactly. It closes. CROSSING TWO, does the warrant repair close this row's specimens: for specimen (ii)'s identifier-absence read as capability-absence there IS a condition -- no renames occurred -- so a warrant demanding it refuses the unsound reading. It closes too. Both repairs close both classes, and DESIGN section 4b keys rows on the repair, so this is ONE class and the separate row was a second authority for a fact this row already owned. **WHAT THE DISSOLVED ROW CONTRIBUTES, AND IT IS NOT NOTHING: A NAME FOR THE CONDITION AND A CEILING ARGUMENT THIS ROW LACKED.** The recognition rule above fires at READING TIME and asks a reader to establish resolution; it has no vocabulary for a resolution that is CORRECT NOW AND LAPSES LATER, because C is stateful while naming a subject is static. The rungs enumerated above are all POSITIONAL and all repaired by a better reader. C is repaired by a CONSTRUCTION: state P, Q, C, the population, and C'S STANDING -- which authority asserts it and what happens when it lapses. Where C's standing resolves to review, memory, habit, or a merge somebody did earlier, it is UNOWNED, and an unowned condition lapses through OTHER PEOPLE'S CORRECT WORK, which is the hardest kind to notice because the mechanism that ends it is indistinguishable from ordinary progress. **ONE SENTENCE OF THE DRAFT WAS WRONG AT EVIDENCE GRAIN AND THE CORRECTION IS THE USEFUL PART.** It said the backing is ACCIDENTAL. It is not: the observation genuinely backs P, faithfully and completely, exactly as this row's opening insists. It supplies NO backing whatever for Q absent an equivalence warrant. What is accidental is THE AGREEMENT OF THE ANSWERS -- and P HAPPENED TO EQUAL Q is not P WAS EVIDENCE FOR Q. Collapsing those two is the same substitution this row names, performed on the word EVIDENCE instead of on a set. **THE CEILING IS 4 AND IT IS DEMONSTRATED IN THIS REPOSITORY TODAY, WHICH IS WHY IT IS NOT ASPIRATION.** `gunbc.diff_baseline`'s `DiffBaseline` arm `ExactReplayBoundary` carries `observed_relation: GitObservedMergeBase` beside its base and head object identities, produced by `gunbc.git_diff_change_window`, consumed through `v2.workflow.floor_diff_observe`, with enrolled witnesses in `dag/test/claim/diff_baseline_witness_test.dag`. Its own note states the rule: the comparison is direct two-dot and NOT triple-dot BECAUSE THE PRODUCER ALREADY CLAIMS THE MERGE-BASE BOUNDARY. That field IS C, carried in the binding's own type, so the two-dot answer cannot be minted without the warrant that makes it answer Q. NEXT-RUNG TRIGGER, AND THE PAIRING IS THE WHOLE TRIGGER: a subject-bound typed equivalence warrant TOGETHER WITH an enumerating consumer over instrument-to-question bindings. The construction half exists today for exactly ONE pair, in one module; nothing ranges over such bindings asking which carry a warrant. Naming either half alone would satisfy the trigger while the capability stayed dead, which is the section 4b(3) defect. **THE ARGUMENT FOR THE PAIRING IS MADE BY THAT ASYMMETRY RATHER THAN BY ASSERTION: a repository can hold the correct construction and still be blind to the class, because a construction nobody enumerates protects exactly the one binding whose author happened to think of it.** **A FOURTH RESOLUTION-AXIS RECEIPT, CROSS-SECTIONAL RATHER THAN ANCESTRAL OR LEXICAL, contributed by tidy-swift-334 from their own retracted finding.** P was a success-and-failure partition of CI runs by HOST FAMILY; Q was stable per-family provisioning capability; the unheld condition was that transient runner state stayed fixed across the sample. The partition was published as a fleet-wide fact and was refuted when THE SAME HOST CHANGED SIDES while host identity stayed constant -- so the instrument had resolved to an adjacent subject, a few minutes of scheduling, and reported faithfully about that. It is the cleanest specimen of the form because the adjacency is neither version nor spelling but SAMPLING WINDOW, and because two disjoint sets of four hosts are exactly what an intermittent defect looks like when each side samples different minutes. **A SECOND INSTANCE IN A DIFFERENT SUBSYSTEM, carried compactly: the required floor's `planned=` delta read as proof that new witnesses ENROLLED.** Two arms, 2026-08-22, each cited by naming its producer -- `report_required_floor_outcome` over `v1_compiler.cli_run` `RequiredFloorOutcome`. Run 32553383403 at e82653fc1, two attempts of one sha on two runners, shows the counters agree. The commit pair af719cc6 and 951683ae, whose whole diff is one `.md` and ONE added line in `src/v2/lens/inert_carrier.dag`, shows that same `offered` counter MOVES while the decline counters stay identical, so the movement is new discovery and not re-classification. DETERMINISM IS NOT INDEPENDENCE: an instrument can be exactly reproducible per commit and still unable to isolate the change under study, and an author who verifies the first and concludes the second has checked the half that was never in doubt.
- **a conjunct that executes, is reached, is gated on, and still discriminates nothing** (a predicate wired into a live check runs on every call, sits inside a gate that really blocks, and yet deletes cleanly: remove it and every witness stays green, because no fixture ever constructs the population that would make it false. **It is invisible precisely BECAUSE reachability is satisfied.** The usual signature of an unenforced mechanism -- nothing calls it -- is absent, so every reachability-based tell reports health, and review reads the conjunct as a wall because it genuinely is evaluated. Executed and enforcing are different properties, and no amount of coverage over the calling path distinguishes them. **It is bounded against three neighbours and none of their recognition rules will find it.** `reachability_read_as_occupancy` asks whether to DELETE a guard that is quiet, and warns that mechanism-yes / join-yes / occupancy-zero is a healthy guard; this class is not about deletion and not about occupancy -- the conjunct here should stay, and the defect is that it was believed to be doing work it was not. Section 6's inert-lens tell is machinery that nothing GATES on; this is gated on. Section 4b's decoration case is a check whose RED cannot be authored ANYWHERE; this RED can be authored -- it simply never was, which is the whole difference, and it is the difference between a permanent ceiling and one missing fixture. **THE REMEDY HAS TWO HALVES AND THE SECOND IS WHAT MAKES THE FIRST SOUND.** First: for each wall, run the DELETE arm and confirm EXACTLY ONE witness reddens -- not that a witness reddens. Second: the subject that isolates the conjunct must be chosen with care, because a red for the wrong reason is how this class survives its own test. **A THIRD HALF, AND IT IS THE MIRROR OF THE SECOND (amended 2026-09-03, tidy-koi-619 on gunbc#10257, found by warm-seal-35): CONFIRM THE DELETE ARM ACTUALLY DELETED.** The second half guards a RED for the wrong reason; this guards a GREEN for the wrong reason, and the remedy as written above is satisfiable by a no-op. A probe that FAILED TO MUTATE and a probe whose mutation the check TOLERATED print the identical passing value, so 'run the delete arm and read the verdict' cannot distinguish a wall that holds from a wall that was never tested. THE PROBE MUST ASSERT THAT IT MUTATED -- count the substitutions, or diff the tree -- as a claim SEPARATE from what the check then says, and then restore and re-run to confirm the green returns, because a one-sided probe does not establish that the verdict tracks the mutation at all. SPECIMEN, and it is worth recording because both failure paths are real rather than imagined: proving the discriminating red for every_restored_stall_is_still_rostered took THREE attempts and the first two mutated nothing. Attempt one ran the edit through `python3 -c` under DOUBLE QUOTES, so the shell consumed the escape sequences and the replacement matched no text. Attempt two chose an anchor that matched TWICE -- the check's own pin list had just been added to the same module as the roster and repeated the same member spellings, which is a general hazard: ANY CHECK THAT ENUMERATES ROSTER MEMBERS CREATES A SECOND POPULATION WITH THE SAME SPELLINGS, so every later sweep over that module must distinguish them. Both attempts printed the passing value and would have been read as the wall holding. Only the third, anchored on roster-only context, dropped the row and produced the red, with the green returning after restore. THE CATCH WAS INCIDENTAL, WHICH IS THE ARGUMENT FOR WRITING IT DOWN: the no-ops were noticed only because the edit script happened to carry an occurrence-count assertion written for an unrelated reason. Nothing in the remedy required it. **SPECIMEN, with both halves executed** (gunbc#9838, `gunbc.self_host_compile_phase_frontier`): `census_population_is_duplicate_free` was added as a conjunct of `receipt_population_coherent`, evaluated on every coherence check and reachable from the live path; deleting it left the whole witness module green. The fixture that isolates it is a receipt whose `refused_files` REPEATS A PATH -- enrolled as `a_receipt_whose_refused_population_repeats_a_path_is_refused_by_coherence` in `test.claim.self_host_compile_phase_frontier_witness`, with `the_same_receipt_without_the_repeat_is_coherent` as its positive control. The subject that looks equally good and is not: a repeated ERROR DIAGNOSTIC IDENTITY also reddens the module, but it breaks a count invariant one line over rather than this conjunct, so it would have scored the DEAD conjunct as LIVE and closed the investigation with the wall still inert. **RUNG: mechanically preventable, and the honest ceiling is the same rung rather than higher.** The unenforcing conjunct stays writable; what exists is an executed falsifier per wall, not a construction that forbids authoring one. The next-rung trigger is a CAPABILITY and not an artifact: a mechanism that derives, from the check itself, the population its removal would change -- so that a conjunct with an empty such population fails to compile rather than waiting for an author to think of the delete arm. Enumerating delete arms by hand does not retire it; it only executes this row's recognition rule once per wall. **Its near twin is `instrument_output_read_as_subject_content`, and they meet from opposite sides**: there a report is complete for the REPORTER and read as complete for the SUBJECT; here a conjunct is genuinely executed and read as genuinely discriminating. Both are the shape where the health signal the reader actually checks -- completeness there, execution here -- is fully satisfied while the property they believe it establishes does not hold. **Neither is found by checking harder along the axis that already reads green.** **SECOND SPECIMEN, 2026-09-02, the same shape one layer down and found by a different tell.** `v1.stage0.required_regen_host` `admit_stage_execution_from_model` built ONE identity list from the stage plan and passed it as BOTH the `planned` and the `executed` field of `RegenStageExecutionObservation`, so `v2.workflow.regen_convergence_transaction` `regen_identity_population_eq` evaluated x == x.clone() on every install of the self-host convergence transaction. The conjunct ran, the install boundary really blocked, and `StagePlannedExecutedMismatch` stayed authorable in `v2.test.claim.regen_convergence_transaction_witness_test` while being unreachable from the real acceptance path -- the exact split section 4b's authorable-RED rule exists to catch. **THE DELETE ARM IS NOT WHAT FOUND IT; THE PRODUCER COUNT IS**, and that is this specimen's addition to the row: reading the two sides of one population equality back to their PRODUCERS and finding a single producer is cheaper than removing the conjunct and re-running, and it needs no experiment -- any equality whose two arguments trace to one value is this class already. **THE REPAIR IS A SECOND PRODUCER, NEVER A SECOND READ OF THE FIRST**: a digest delta over the journalled generated roster, taken before the copy loop and again after the build, carried in its own Rust newtype `ObservedEffectPopulation` with no conversion from the plan, so a later refactor cannot re-collapse the two sides without deleting a type. Its discriminating red `stage_execution_joins_the_plan_to_independently_observed_effects` carries TWO arms because one-directional identity checking is how the loss survived: an effect on a path OUTSIDE the plan, which was green before because `allowed_after` blanket-permits every already-dirty stage0 path for the rest of the transaction; and a planned surface the stage left byte-identical, which did refuse before but as `InstalledDigestMismatch` -- a CONTENT verdict standing in for a POPULATION one, so the arm asserts the cause and not that it refused.)
- **unbacked execution claim** (prose asserts a live executing relation — this runs on X, X reads these fields, Y already resolves Z — that no authority backs, and a reader forms the premise and plans against it. §4b's CI rung drop names the harm PREMISE CONTAMINATION in its own words; this row is that harm as a general class, since the drop is about one paragraph in the canonical authority and the class is about every carrier. **THE ORIGINS ARE A FIELD OF THIS ONE ROW RATHER THAN SEPARATE ROWS, because the recognition rule is identical across them** — resolve the claim against the authority that owns the relation — **but the REMEDY BRANCHES ON THE ORIGIN, and a remedy applied without reading the origin manufactures new false statements.** Three origins are ATTESTED here and a fourth is conceivable but was NOT found. ORIGIN 1, EXECUTOR DELETED: the claim was true and its executor was removed. Specimen: `gunbc.floor_component_receipt_document` recited that an alert downloads an artifact named `floor-component-receipt` from a run id it is given and reads four named fields; that alert was `falsifier-alert.yml`, deleted 2026-08-15, and a lane read the module, believed the transport live, planned against it, and was corrected by its reviewing authority — the cost is measured, not hypothetical. Second specimen, same origin: `gunbc.ci_failure_class` stated its dissolution trigger as calling `classify_failure_reason` *the way claim_executor already resolves* `gunbc.floor_component_receipt`; `write_floor_component_receipt_at` did exactly that from 2026-07-30 (gunbc#7467) until gunbc#9228 deleted it on 2026-08-25. ORIGIN 2, THE CITED SYMBOL DIED WHILE ITS CONTENT SURVIVED AS AN ANNOTATION, and this one has a corpus-wide producer: `v1_compiler.cli_run` twice cited `floor_component_resource_checkpoint_note` and `floor_component_phase_journal_scaffold_note`, which were real `data …: String` declarations in `gunbc.floor_component_receipt` until the 2026-08-30 prose-bankruptcy sweep (gunbc#9752) converted module-scope commentary rows into §4c annotations corpus-wide. §4c makes an annotation uncitable by construction — `v1_compiler.cli_run` `annotation_erased_scan_text` feeds the semantic passes, so annotation text is not present in the tree the citation harvester walks. **THE CONSEQUENCE IS THE OPPOSITE OF THE ONE THIS ROW FIRST DREW, AND THE CORRECTION IS THE INSTRUCTIVE PART.** The first revision reasoned that the sweep therefore broke every citation of a converted row, making the population the sweep's own diff. Measured by a separate lane (gunbc XL-0-CITE, 2026-09-01) against `v1_compiler.declaration_index`, whose only citation producers are `citation_from_record_literal` and `citation_from_constructor_call`: the typed-citation breakage is **ZERO**, computed both today and as of the sweep commit, on a control that discriminates in both directions. A plain name in annotation prose was never a citation, so the sweep could not break one. What that leaves is a REAL class with an honest ceiling rather than a defect population: these citations bind READERS and no mechanism, so they sit in §4b's *outside the modeled guarantee* column — observed and repaired by reading, never gated — and calling them a dangling-citation population would have been rung inflation about a check that by construction cannot see them. The two `cli_run` sites are genuine instances of the harm and there is no mechanical census that would have found them. ORIGIN 3, WRONG AUTHORITY FOR SOMETHING THAT STILL EXECUTES: `dag/test/claim/instrument_sandbox_witness_test` says its live half `runs on the falsifier lane`; that half sits on `FalsifierSubstrateLongLane`, which `std.witness_admission` `witness_cadence_has_scheduled_route` reports routeless — but it also carries a local recipe, and `OfflineLocalRecipe` HAS a route, so the relation is live and only the named authority is wrong. THE FOURTH, NEVER BACKED AT ALL, IS NOT ATTESTED IN THIS CORPUS and is recorded as unmeasured rather than as a population. **THIS ROW IS NOT THE AUTHORITY ON ANY POPULATION, and saying so is the point:** `gunbc.deleted_cadence_reference_census` already owns the falsifier-cadence instance of origin 1 — twelve sites, a `DeletedCadenceReferenceStanding` vocabulary whose `PremiseInvalidated` arm is exactly this class, and one `GuaranteeRungDrop` filed once for the shared executor rather than per site. A pattern row that restated its population would be a second authority for a fact that already has one, which is the §3 violation this row's own remedy is meant to prevent; so the census is cited here and nothing about its contents is copied. What belongs HERE is only the recognition rule and the remedy arms, which generalize past the falsifier. **THE MECHANICAL TEST for finding one is sharper than asking whether the verb is runs or is enrolled: resolve the claim against the authority that owns the relation, and ask whether the prose implies a live route for something that authority says has none.** The naive runs/enrolled discriminator loses one arm and it is the common one — `Enrolled on falsifier_rehomed_bin_wet_entries (nightly batch 5)` is nominally an enrollment claim, which is TRUE, but `nightly` asserts a cadence that executes and `FalsifierRehomedBinWet` is routeless, so the reader forms the false premise anyway. Three outcomes per line: asserts execution (contaminating), asserts enrollment only (true, leave it), asserts enrollment while implying a live cadence (contaminating). **THE REMEDY HAS THREE ARMS, KEYED TO WHAT THE AUTHORITY SAYS NOW, and picking the wrong one is itself an instance of this class.** Origin 1 → PAST TENSE WITH THE DATE, keeping the reasoning, because the reasoning usually outlives its executor and a reader who finds a dangling claim silently deleted learns nothing. Origin 2 → name the module and the FACT rather than the dead symbol, since §4c left no symbol to cite; past-tensing here would wrongly report the content as gone when only its citability is. Origin 3 → CORRECT IT to the authority that does back it, NOT past tense: past-tensing a live relation records it as dead and is a new contamination in the opposite direction, produced by the remedy itself. Origin 4 → say it was never backed, or delete the assertion while keeping any surviving reasoning; NEVER past tense, because there is no past to record, and `X USED TO resolve Y` asserts an execution that also never happened. **The default against deletion is a default and not an absolute: a sentence whose entire content is a false execution claim has no reasoning to preserve.** **THE SELF-RECEIPT, and it is the most useful thing in this row (2026-09-01):** the first revision of this entry asserted the `ci_failure_class` clause and the two `cli_run` notes as never-true, on the strength of a grep showing they resolve nowhere TODAY. **Both were true when written**, and the row about unbacked claims therefore contained two of them. It was caught by a reviewing authority asking for verification rather than inheritance, not by any check. **RECOGNITION RULE EARNED: `resolves nowhere now` is not `never resolved`, the distinction is decided by history and not by the working tree, and the instrument is `git log --all -S` over the DECLARATION FORM** — which also separates origin 1 from origin 2 by showing WHAT deleted the referent. The neighbouring rule is `positional_citation`'s: a citation left naming a deleted symbol fakes a grep hit, so every arm above records the change rather than quietly removing the name.)
- **a source-ingested module has no judged standing** (the required gates adjudicate entry-derived closures, while source-root ingest admits a larger identity population; a module outside every required closure can carry a structurally checkable defect while every gate stays green. This is NOT an import-edge class: globally unique names can resolve without a direct import, so authored inbound imports neither prove nor disprove judgment. It is also NOT a claim that every identity outside the closures is defective or should be gated. The severity is unknown, and that unknowability is the finding. **SPECIMEN AND DISCRIMINATING CONTROL** (node://adhoc-2d3c27b7-660, PR #9899): gunbc.annotation_parse_instrument_agreement constructed Terminal { reason, authority } although Terminal declares only reason; a clean required run left both build and witnesses green and both in-run judgment receipts omitted the module. The matched visible control placed the same undeclared-field construction in ordinary production module gunbc.clock_read: both required lanes refused with `field 'authority' not found in type 'LaneVisibilityControl'`; removing only the plant returned both lanes to green. Thus visibility, with defect class held constant, discriminates the arms. The witnesses prediction was weaker than its result: only submitted-closure membership was known beforehand, yet execution established strict judgment by refusing. **MEASUREMENT, EXPLICITLY NOT COVERAGE OR ENFORCEMENT:** the producer joins every first-party identity admitted by the exact source-root ingest against the union of module identities for which build or witnesses produced a strict typecheck verdict. On clean run 33484666539 it derived 4,419 admitted, 947 build-judged, 2,158 witnesses-judged, 2,319 in the judgment union, and 2,100 with no required-lane judgment. Build contributes only 161 identities beyond witnesses, so the two required closures are far from independent and their union remains close to one lane's reach. Exactly one of the 2,100 is confirmed orphaned by the executed control; the other 2,099 are Undecided because no typed authority currently says whether each is an executing standalone entry, fixture/generated/excluded, retired, or orphaned. Those identities are deliberately re-derived and NOT committed as a baseline or roster: a hand-carried 2,099-row exemption set would be a ratchet wearing a wall's name. The instrument reports; it does not refuse, and citing it as a gate is rung inflation. **PROVENANCE LIMIT:** in-run strict judgments and cross-process content-addressed cache judgments are separate columns. Required CI does not arm GUNBC_RESOLVED_GRAPH_CACHE_DIR, so the cross-process column was empty beside nonempty in-run columns in both planted and reverted runs. That empty column is a control for the unarmed premise, NOT evidence that the provenance split is exercised by required CI. **CEILING: decidable but waiting on a single standing authority.** A wall now would either false-red legitimate importer-free subjects or grandfather opaque debt. The next rung requires four capabilities together: (1) one typed standing registry exhaustively binds every first-party ingest identity to required judgment, a verified executing consumer, typed fixture/generated/exclusion, retirement, or orphan standing; (2) each executing-consumer binding is checked against the named consumer's actual roster and execution receipt, so a hollow alias cannot admit it; (3) a changed retained identity resolves only through its named consumer from materialized facts within the required floor's 8-second wall and 500-millisecond CPU envelopes, never by whole-corpus compilation; and (4) the two-direction matched undeclared-field control remains executable. At that rung admission makes the invalid state unwritable; until then this remains measurement and an Undecided population, not a defect backlog.)
- refusal deferred to the emitted runtime (a compiler refusal expressed as a construct IN THE EMITTED PROGRAM rather than as a diagnostic in the COMPILING one, so the compile reports success and the line does not stop). INVALID STATE: an emission path that cannot answer a declared fact writes a panic, an error expression or a stub into the artifact and returns normally, so the compiler emits N files and reports zero diagnostics beside a body that cannot run. HARM: fabricated plausible output at the strongest grain -- an artifact that looks like a compiled program and is not one. The refusal message may be perfectly honest and still fail DESIGN section 5, because nobody reads it until production; the reviewer's three questions are does the line stop, is the stop typed and located, does analysis precede restart, and this arm fails the first. DISTINGUISHING FACTS: the tell is a SUCCESS REPORT beside a refusal construct in the same run -- 'compiled: 7 files emitted, 0 diagnostics' printed by a compile whose emitted body was a panic naming its own refusal. It is NOT the same as a legitimate runtime arm at an honest boundary: the discriminator is whether the compiler KNEW at emit time, from modeled structure, that the artifact could not answer. If it knew and wrote the knowledge into the output instead of the diagnostic stream, the class applies. SPECIMEN AND HOW IT WAS FOUND: gunbc#9886's shell service-emission climb refused an unrealizable output channel by emitting panic!() from emit_shell_channel_expr while emit_artifact returned its files normally. Found by a DIFFERENTIAL READ against an independent implementation of the same repair (calm-boar-314's superseded WIP), then by execution on a probe; five consecutive reviews APPROVED the arm, two of them praising the refusal by name, so review did not catch it and is not the mechanism that will. WHY REVIEW MISSES IT: the refusal is present, typed, located and honestly worded at the point a reader's eye lands, and the missing half is an ABSENCE elsewhere -- no diagnostic in a stream the diff does not show. RECOGNITION RULE: for every arm that emits a refusal construct into a target artifact, ask what the COMPILE reports on the same input. If the answer is success, or any file count above zero, the arm belongs to this class. REPAIR, and it is usually already written next door: pair the refusal expression with a blocking emit-stage diagnostic so the artifact producer returns no files. In the specimen the FILE transport already had that pair and shell had the expression half only, so the repair was building the twin rather than inventing a path -- which is the general shape, since a codebase that emits refusals at all usually has one arm that does it correctly. KEEP THE EXPRESSION as the unreachable second half rather than deleting it, so the two readers cannot disagree if the wall is ever weakened. RUNG FOUND AT: 1, mitigatable -- a runtime panic in generated code, established by executing the emitter and reading its own success line, NOT by reading the type. CEILING: structurally guaranteed, and reached in the specimen -- a located compile-time refusal per declared field, derived from modeled structure, with zero files emitted. NEXT TRIGGER for the class as a whole: an emission-stage census that pairs every refusal-construct emitter with its diagnostic twin, since nothing today makes the missing half visible and the specimen was found by accident. POPULATION NOTE THE SPECIMEN CARRIES: the wall converted a SILENT corpus defect into a loud one -- gunbc.WitnessBin.Run declares stderr_truncated, stderr_total_bytes and stderr_retained_bytes, three channels the Rust realization cannot answer because the emitted Command body implements no capture policy, so every entry closure reaching that operation now refuses at field grain. That was always true and the old arm said nothing. Measured OUTSIDE REQUIRED COVERAGE, which is the honest phrasing and not 'affects nothing': claim_executor --required-emit-compile is green on all 8 rostered entries, each with its own mutation-established red, those 8 reaching 47 modules, with ZERO shell-channel diagnostics.)
- **mitigation injected where the judgment declined** (a downstream stage injects a RUNTIME mitigation at exactly the seam where an upstream judgment returned Undecidable, so a 4b rung-1 fallback comes to occupy the place the wall belongs and the declined seam stops looking empty. **THE LOAD-BEARING HALF IS WHAT THE COINCIDENCE PROVES ABOUT THE DECLINE**: an Undecidable verdict is normally read as benign conservatism — the facts did not settle it, nothing is claimed, no harm asserted — and a mitigation landing on the SAME LINE is the first evidence that a real defect was sitting under that silence. The decline is therefore load-bearing AT THIS SITE, which refutes reading an Undecidable verdict as evidence of ABSENCE. **IT DOES NOT MAKE THE DECLINE CENSUS PREDICTIVE, and this row carries that limit because the author first wrote the stronger claim and then measured it FALSE** on the same board that produced the specimen: 259 of 272 declines sit in modules with ZERO blocking errors, only 6 of 28 decline-bearing modules carry any error at all, and the module holding 42 of the 63 errors carries ZERO declines and 105 ACCEPTS. Declines and defects are ANTI-CORRELATED at module grain, so this specimen is ONE co-location out of 272 and a walk of the decline census would be walking sites that are ~95% empty. The class is about a mitigation landing on a declined seam; it is NOT a claim that declines predict defects. Distinct from `absorbing_fallback`, whose arm WIDENS to a superset and destroys the precise mechanism s signal; here the arm NARROWS to a panic and destroys nothing except the evidence that a judgment was owed. Distinct from `reflection_evidence_structural_proof`, which mistakes an observation for a proof; here nothing is proven or observed — a decision was declined and then papered. Specimen with a receipt, measured 2026-09-01 on gunbc 0e8c49d and unrepaired at authoring: `v2.std.diagnostic outcome_accepted` is `fn outcome_accepted<T>(value: T) -> Outcome<T>` with T FREE; `v2.compiler.07_target_carriers target_fidelity_quotient_optional` declares `-> Outcome<Optional<Node>>` and calls `outcome_accepted(Present { value: child })`, so T instantiates to `Optional<Node>` and THE SOURCE IS CORRECT. The Rust emitter nonetheless rendered `Some(child).expect("fail-closed: an optional value flowed into non-optional parameter 0 of outcome_accepted (empty Optional at runtime)")`. FOUR DEFECTS IN ONE LINE: it fires on a GENERIC formal where `Optional<Node>` is a legitimate instantiation rather than a cardinality escape, so the check does not know T is free; it substitutes a runtime panic for a compile-time judgment; the emitted code does NOT typecheck anyway (rustc E0308 expected `Option<Rc<Node>>` found `Rc<Node>`), so the mitigation buys nothing while standing where the wall belongs; and its own panic string calls itself `fail-closed`, which is 4b rung inflation inside a string literal — a diagnostic naming the discipline it violates is worse than a silent one because it will be cited as evidence the discipline holds. The coincidence that makes this a class and not a bug: `v1.compiler.infer declared_type_inhabitance` returned InhabitanceUndecidable{UndecidableOptionalCarrier} at that module s ONLY optional-carrier decline, 07_target_carriers.dag:128:24 parameter `value` — the same line the emitter mitigated. RUNG FOUND AT: 1 (mitigatable) on a seam whose CEILING is 3 (structurally guaranteed), since generic-formal instantiation is decidable from modeled structure. CONSEQUENCE WITH ITS OWN TRIGGER, deliberately NOT folded into this row: wiring the optional-carrier judgment is argued by this specimen but is a separate change, and its trigger is a producer at that seam that can distinguish a FREE type variable from a declared non-optional formal — until that exists the judgment would decline for the same reason it declines today.)
- **a transport close is read as protocol completion** (a consumer of a streamed response decides the response is FINISHED because the connection ENDED, rather than because the protocol's own terminator arrived. Every partial answer then presents as a complete one, and the consumer reports success while holding a fragment. This is state-space conflation on the completion axis specifically -- `the bytes stopped` and `the sender said it was done` are two facts, and a stream has a third state, SEVERED, that a two-state reading cannot express. **SPECIMEN, measured 2026-09-01 by a peer session on its own routing layer.** A streaming SSE client accumulated `message_start`, then a long run of `content_block_delta`, and registered completion in `res.on('end')` UNCONDITIONALLY -- never checking that `content_block_stop`, `message_delta` carrying `stop_reason`, and `message_stop` had arrived. A stream severed after 364 thinking deltas therefore produced a well-formed response object containing an intro line and a heading, which the transcript recorded with a tidy `stop_reason` and the UI rendered faithfully as the model's answer. **EVERY LAYER DOWNSTREAM OF THE READING LAYER BEHAVED CORRECTLY, AND THAT IS WHY THE TRUNCATION PROPAGATED SILENTLY.** The reading layer did NOT behave correctly -- it is the defect, converting a transport termination into a protocol completion and minting a `stop_reason` no sender had emitted. What every layer BELOW it then did was faithful: the transcript recorded the object it was handed, and the UI rendered that object exactly. The distinction is the whole diagnostic value of the row -- a defect that hands its consumers a WELL-FORMED artifact recruits every correct layer beneath it into carrying the wrong answer, so correctness downstream is not evidence of correctness upstream. It is also why the class survived a full evening of investigation that cleared the UI, the parser, the token budget, and the serving stack in turn: each of those really was innocent, and each exoneration made the true location look less likely rather than more. **THE DIAGNOSIS WAS INVERTED FOR HOURS BY THE ARTIFACT THE DEFECT ITSELF PRODUCED.** The fabricated `stop_reason: end_turn` was read at face value as `the model chose to stop`, so the investigation was pointed at the model and at long-context serving -- a lead reinforced by a genuinely large KV allocation -- while the defect sat in the layer doing the reading. **RECOGNITION RULE: find the completion predicate and ask whether it names a PROTOCOL EVENT or a TRANSPORT EVENT.** `on('end')`, `on('close')`, process exit, EOF, a read returning zero, and `the socket closed` are transport; a terminator frame, a `done` member, a declared length satisfied, and a trailer are protocol. A consumer whose completion predicate is transport-shaped cannot distinguish a finished answer from a severed one, no matter what it does downstream. **THE SECOND HALF OF THE RULE IS ABOUT RECOVERY, AND IT IS WHY THE CLASS IS NOT MERELY A MISSING CHECK.** Neither the Messages API nor Ollama's compatibility layer carries a resumption protocol -- no sequence numbers, no cursor, no reattachable session -- so a severed stream cannot be resumed and the only recovery is re-issuing the whole request. A client that cannot DETECT truncation therefore cannot retry either, and the missing predicate silently removes the entire recovery arm rather than one diagnostic. **THE REMEDY IS ORDERED AND THE ORDER IS LOAD-BEARING**: make truncation a typed refusal FIRST, because until it is detectable a retry cannot be triggered and a fix cannot be verified; only then add re-issue. Adding retry first produces a client that retries the errors it can already see and silently accepts the ones it cannot. **GUNBC'S OWN EQUIVALENT PATH IS STRUCTURALLY IMMUNE, and the contrast is the useful part rather than a boast.** `gunbc.spark.serving_readback_parse` `spark_serving_parse_generate_done` reads completion from the response's OWN `done` member, and `spark_serving_parse_version_json` refuses a body it cannot parse with `SparkServingReadbackRefused` carrying a typed cause instead of collapsing to an absent reading. A severed body there produces a refusal, not a short answer -- the protocol terminator is the predicate, which is exactly the repair the specimen needs. **BOUNDED AGAINST NEIGHBOURS.** Against `empty_observation_narrow`: there an observation that could not express what changed renders as `nothing is affected`; here a PARTIAL observation renders as a COMPLETE one, so the count is plausible rather than empty and no zero draws attention. Against `absorbing_fallback`: that arm WIDENS on ignorance and is merely expensive; this arm reports a fragment as an answer and is silently wrong, which is below the ladder rather than on it. Against `unbacked_execution_claim`: there prose asserts a relation no authority backs; here a machine-generated artifact asserts a completion no protocol event backs, and it is more dangerous precisely because a `stop_reason` field reads as evidence.)
- **merge region excludes the shared tail** (a roster carrier whose rows are multi-line blocks ending in an identical suffix makes every two-lane append resolve WRONG BY DEFAULT. The three-way merge factors the shared suffix out of the conflict region, so the region holds two half-blocks above the markers and one tail below them, and the purely additive resolution -- delete the markers, keep both sides, which is the CORRECT resolution when the two appended rows are independent -- leaves the first row's closing lines belonging to the second. Specimen: this carrier, which conflicted on every integration of main across four merge bases in one session (2026-09-01) with a shared tail of `evidence: [],` and `}`; `gunbc.rung_drop` had the same shape with `}` alone. **THE CLASS IS LOUD, AND THAT IS THE HALF A REPORT OF IT WILL GET WRONG.** The first reading was that the severed row survives as a structurally broken declaration under which the natural check -- declared names against rostered names -- still passes, because both lists stay complete. Measured against a gunbc built at d0009ca531 on the resolved shape: it is a PARSE REFUSAL at the module index, `expected expression, found Eq`, because a `data` keyword cannot stand in record-field position. Nothing reaches the checks that reading worried about, so the cost is toil and not silent wrongness -- a load-bearing authority whose every integration lands a conflict whose natural resolution does not compile, hand-repaired each time. **RECOGNITION RULE: for a carrier two lanes append to, ask what suffix the appended units SHARE, because a conflict region never contains it -- whatever semantics live in that suffix are exactly what a resolution can drop.** THE REPAIR IS THE UNIT AND NOT A CHECK: make the appended unit one line, and the shared suffix is a blank separator carrying nothing.  **SECOND, INDEPENDENT INSTANCE, AND IT RE-RAN THE MISREPORT THIS ROW WARNS ABOUT** (vivid-boar-481, 2026-09-01, integrating main on a later base than the one above): the severed identity was `sealing_property_erases_structure` and the recipient of its donated tail was `accepted_source_emits_uncompilable_target`, over the same `evidence: [],` and `}` suffix. The finder produced the naive both-sides resolution, spotted the fusion by AUDITING THE RESULTING DECLARATIONS rather than by any marker, repaired it -- and then reported that the fused file STILL PARSED and that nothing went red. That report was an inference from reading the text, never an execution, and compiling the reconstructed fused shape refused at `expected expression, found Eq`, the same diagnostic recorded above. So the row's corrected reading survived contact with a second lane, and the reading it corrects -- silent survival -- is what the second reporter independently reinvented before measuring. THE TRANSFERABLE PART IS THAT THE MISREPORT IS THE DEFAULT AND NOT A LAPSE: a reader who resolves the conflict, sees both rows present and no markers, and does not compile, will conclude 'parses fine' every time, because every visible signal available WITHOUT executing agrees with that conclusion. The audit that finds the fusion and the execution that establishes its cost are two different steps, and doing only the first is what produces a confident wrong report about severity.)
- **a construction-restricting property read as a structure-erasing one** (a declaration is annotated to RESTRICT how its inhabitants may be built, and a consumer that asks about the declaration's SHAPE treats the annotation as a reason to stop looking -- so the deliberate climb on the construction axis silently drops a rung on every axis decided by shape, with no declaration, because the two axes are read by different consumers. **THE INVERSION IS THE CLASS**: the annotation exists to make the carrier MORE distinct from its payload, and the consumer's response is to make it INDISTINGUISHABLE from everything. Recognition rule, keyed on the SHAPE and not on any one keyword: whenever a declaration-level marker is stored in a general side-channel -- a properties list, an attribute bag, a modifier set -- find every consumer that BRANCHES ON THAT CHANNEL BEING NON-EMPTY rather than on the specific marker, and ask what each of them would have answered had the marker been absent; a consumer that answers 'opaque', 'unknown', or 'skip' for the whole channel has conflated 'this declaration carries a marker I do not model' with 'this declaration has no structure', and every future marker inherits the same silence on arrival. **SPECIMEN, with the discriminating pair** (gunbc XL-0-FLOOR, 2026-09-01): `v2.compiler.normalized_tree` `NormalizedTree` was sealed by BL-1 from an alias for `Node` into a `sole_constructor` record so that a wrapper-retention drop would be unwritable. `sole_constructor` is carried as a PROPERTY on the declaration node (`v1.compiler.parse` `parsed_sole_constructor_properties`), and `v1.compiler.infer` `exposure_view_for_node` answered `OpaqueTypeHead` for any node whose properties list was non-empty -- so `nominal_product_head_name` answered the empty string for every sealed carrier, `nominal_product_inhabitance_refusal` short-circuited on the empty head, and `declared_type_inhabitance` fell through to `Inhabits`. `v2.compiler.source_authority` `normalized_source_ast_equal_witness` then bound a `NormalizedTree` at a formal declared `Node` and the compiler ACCEPTED it: the ordinary-floor violation DESIGN section 4b names as exact application bijection, below baseline. The discriminating pair is two record declarations identical to the token except for the word `sole_constructor` -- the sealed one compiled with zero diagnostics where the plain one refused. **WHY A CENSUS MISSED IT, which is the part that generalizes**: gunbc#8886 measured 285 sites behind the `v2.*` direct-call argument-type exemption, of which 148 were `Node` against `ResolvedTree`/`ParseTree` and siblings -- every one a transparent-ALIAS false positive. This shape never appears in that roster and could not have, because it is a TRUE positive the comparison relation cannot reach: the census enumerates what the relation REFUSES, so a defect the relation is blind to is invisible to the census by construction, and a large census reads as thorough coverage of exactly the region it cannot see. **THE MISDIAGNOSIS THIS CLASS INVITES, recorded because this lane was dispatched with it**: the silence sat inside a module the exemption covers, so the exemption was the obvious cause; refuting it took running the identical program in an ordinary module and on a tree with the exemption deleted, both of which stayed silent, while the same run showed the exemption's effect on a kernel red so the arms were provably live. A plausible nearby mechanism that is genuinely broken is the most expensive wrong answer available, because repairing it changes nothing and the repair reads as coverage. **Rung: mechanically preventable, and the ceiling for this class as a class is the same rung.** The repair makes one marker structure-preserving by positive establishment -- the parser builds the identical declaration node and only the properties list differs -- and every OTHER marker in that channel stays opaque and unmeasured, which is a named residue rather than a covered class. The next-rung trigger is a CAPABILITY: markers carried in the declaration's own type rather than in an untyped side-channel, so that a consumer branching on the channel cannot compile.)
- **a two-reader disagreement census is blind to the case where BOTH readers are wrong together** (an instrument whose subject is DISAGREEMENT between two producers of one answer reports AGREEMENT as healthy, so the population where both answer the same WRONG thing is scored green and is invisible to it by construction. The trap is that such an instrument is commissioned precisely to adjudicate a known-defective population, and it can be structurally incapable of seeing that population while looking perfectly well-formed and producing rows. **SPECIMEN, measured 2026-09-01.** `v1.tests.claim.carrier_realization_census` records, per type-reference occurrence, the legacy short-circuit key and the strict authority answer, and reports where they diverge. Its calibration control -- stated in docs/plans/carrier-realization-arbiter-repair-design.md -- requires the diagnostic-producing `DivergesWithExactIdentity` subset to reproduce arm A 25 sites, joined by source declaration and enclosing declaration, never by line. Run over the transitive import closure of `src/v2/compiler/01_tokenize.dag`, the module that emits the file where every arm-A site lives contributed 76 rows: 74 Agrees, 2 IdentityUnavailable, ZERO divergences, and all 26 of its `String`-named rows Agree. Every divergence row in the closure sat in `v2.std.text`, the DECLARING module, in the opposite direction. Empty intersection with the population the control exists to find. **THE SHAPE OF THE ABSENCE IS THE DIAGNOSIS, and it is what distinguishes this class from an over-broad walk.** `gunbc#9900` predicted the walk might visit occurrences the emitter never renders; that defect shows the target population PLUS extras. This showed the target population NOT AT ALL. Same instrument, same rows, opposite conclusions -- and only the shape separates them. **WHY BOTH READERS AGREE THERE**, read off `v1.compiler.emit_rust` `checkpoint_table_bypasses_identity_note` REACHABILITY CORRECTION rather than executed here: the short-circuit returns the host spelling before consulting any authority, and the authority answers from `type_reference_decl_file`, whose fallback returns the file containing the REFERENCE.
