RELATED: `non_verdict_disposition_surfaces_as_refusal` carries the aggregate-boundary half, and the `gunbc.rung_drop` row `floor_cost_claim_qualification_unavailable` carries the cost half -- neither names the missing purpose join, which is why this is its own row.
- **a repair enumerates its own blast radius by inspection** (INVALID STATE: a change that falsifies a standing claim states a COUNT of the sites it makes false -- in its title, its body, or both -- and that count was never derived from a decidable search over the corpus. THE RECOGNITION RULE IS ABOUT PROVENANCE AND NOT ABOUT THE NUMBER: read the repair for the count, then ask which SEARCH produced it. If no producer is named, the count is an author's recollection of the sites they happened to have open, and the sites they did not have open are the entire population at risk. HARM: the surviving sites are not merely stale prose. They are ASSERTIONS STANDING IN AUTHORITIES, and DESIGN section 5 forbids exactly this -- a wrong answer sitting quietly where a reader will build on it. The count is what makes the reader stop looking, so a repair that under-enumerates is strictly worse than one that enumerates nothing at all. **SPECIMEN, gunbc#10078**, whose title is the class in one line: "Promote rust-unit-tests into the required aggregate: one row, plus THE TWO SENTENCES IT MAKES FALSE". It touched two files and it was right about both. The surviving population was TEN sites across FIVE other authorities, and every one of them was returned by a single decidable search -- `git grep -n -i 'not a .needs. of the required aggregate\|not yet a .needs. of the required aggregate' -- '*.dag' '*.md'` -- which nobody ran -- and a THIRD SPELLING that neither the inspection nor the grep reached: DESIGN's Building & checks bullet describing CI as "two parallel jobs, plus a third aggregating job", which states the same fact by omission and reads as two gating lanes. **THE INSTRUMENT IMPROVED TWICE AND ONLY THE THIRD ONE ANSWERED THE QUESTION, WHICH IS THE REAL RECOGNITION RULE AND IS SHARPER THAN THE ONE THIS ROW WAS COMMISSIONED WITH.** Three counts of one population, in order: #10078 counted BY INSPECTION and got 2; the lane dispatching this repair counted BY GREP and got 10; this lane counted BY MEANING and got 12. The class therefore survives a REAL instrument and is not a story about carelessness -- which matters, because the obvious remedy ("run a grep") is the second step and the second step was still wrong. A PHRASE-KEYED SEARCH OVER PROSE CANNOT ENUMERATE A SEMANTIC POPULATION AT ALL: one fact has unbounded spellings, so such a search returns a LOWER BOUND and reporting its output AS the population is the same move as reporting an inspection count. The dispatching lane committed exactly that, in the dispatch of this row, about forty minutes after writing that a grep is decidable here and the author did not run one -- recorded because a class whose own filing reproduces it is the strongest evidence that it is structural and not personal. **AND THE SEARCH ITSELF UNDER-ENUMERATED BY TWO, WHICH IS THAT SAME STEP MADE CONCRETE.** That grep is keyed on the NEGATED stock sentence, so it cannot see a site that names the same fact AFFIRMATIVELY as a future condition. Two paragraphs in `gunbc.emitted_closure_compile_seed_growth` declare the reversal condition for an `#[ignore]` as "when `rust-unit-tests` is promoted into the required aggregate" -- a condition that HAD FIRED, so those are not stale descriptions but live obligations to re-decide, and they are the two most consequential sites of the twelve. A phrase-keyed search is a lower bound on its own population; the count it returns is evidence, not the answer. **THE THREE GRADES OF SURVIVING SITE NEED THREE DIFFERENT REPAIRS, and conflating them is the second half of this class.** (1) A DECLARED RUNG DROP WHOSE RESTORATION TRIGGER THE CHANGE FIRES. The drop then stands as debt that no longer exists -- `fabricated_debt` is that outcome, and this class is one route into it. The repair is to ADJUDICATE the trigger conjunct by conjunct and retire or restate; it is never to edit the drop's prose. (2) A RUNG CLAIM RESTING ON THE FALSIFIED PREMISE AS ONE OF TWO CLAUSES WHERE ONLY ONE CARRIES THE CONCLUSION. This is the dangerous grade because the conclusion SURVIVES: the text stays true and stops being sound. It now reads as two independent protections when one exists, so a later author who removes the surviving protection -- deleting an `#[ignore]`, widening a scope -- still believes the dead clause covers them and fails open with the sentence agreeing. The repair is to DELETE the false clause rather than soften it, and to state in the same breath that removing the surviving fact is BY ITSELF sufficient to change what the site establishes. (3) ORDINARY DESCRIPTION, corrected in place. Only this grade is find-and-replace, and treating grades (1) and (2) as if they were is how a sweep manufactures a resolving citation that lies. **BOUNDARY AGAINST THE NEIGHBOURS IT WILL SIT BESIDE, drawn so this is not a nickname.** `check_subject_narrower_than_its_declared_claim` is about the range of an EXECUTING CHECK and is invisible from a green result; this is about the range of a REPAIR, its subject is prose, and nothing executes over it at all. `disagreement_census_blind_to_agreed_wrong` needs two readers agreeing wrongly; here there is one author and no second reader. `fabricated_debt` is grade (1)'s outcome, not this class. `stable_citation_mutable_referent` is a citation whose target moved; here the citation resolves fine and the SENTENCE AROUND IT became false. **RUNG, AND IT IS OUTSIDE THE LADDER RATHER THAN LOW.** Nothing executes over this class. Two capabilities block a wall, both named as capabilities rather than as artifacts, and either alone is sufficient to leave it unwalled: (i) A CORPUS-SCALE ANNOTATION READ SURFACE AVAILABLE TO A WITNESS. Six of the specimen's ten sites are `//` annotations, and DESIGN section 4c makes them invisible by construction -- semantic passes receive the annotation-erased projection, an annotation mints no occurrence identity, and the only corpus-reading surfaces this repository has (`v2.std.decl_index` `decl_facts` and `data_decl_type_facts`) are semantic. `std.source_annotation` CAPTURES the rows; nothing serves them to a reader. THE GAP IS NOW PROVEN RATHER THAN ARGUED, AND IN ITS STRONGER FORM: `v2.workflow.required_lane_claim_agreement` is a wall that EXISTS AND WORKS on this population's projected-ledger neighbours -- authored red by the live corpus and greened by the repair -- and it cannot reach the annotation half at all. The six are therefore not merely unchecked; they are unreachable BY A WORKING WALL STANDING BESIDE THEM. That check's range is stated in its own module rather than widened to cover them, because a phrase list extended over an unreadable channel is `check_subject_narrower_than_its_declared_claim` written deliberately. (ii) A LIVE-CORPUS REFLECTION AFFORDABLE PER PULL REQUEST. Even the literal half needs `decl_facts` over the whole corpus, and this repository has ALREADY adjudicated that cost against itself: `test.claim.long.host_identity_single_authoring_witness_test` was moved out of the merge path under a declared drop for "a corpus-scale reflection cost with no lane that both admits it and runs it", restoration trigger "a cadence that executes test/claim/long/, or the decl_facts surface becoming cheap enough for per-PR discovery". Landing a new scanner in that same unexecuted lane is the inert lens DESIGN section 6 calls a lie; landing it on every pull request would overturn a standing decision from inside an unrelated repair lane. UNTIL BOTH LAND, THE ONLY HONEST MECHANISM IS THE AUTHOR RUNNING THE SEARCH, and this row exists so that the search is the reviewer's question rather than the author's discipline: a repair claiming a count must name the producer of that count, exactly as DESIGN section 6 requires of any other measurement.)

A defect that survives its own targeted repair is evidence about the MEDIUM, not about the author's care. AND IT WAS NOT FOUND BY AUDIT: it surfaced from the floor's refusal of an unrelated PR. There, a check EXECUTES, is reached, goes green, and its implementation ranges over less than its declared subject -- the repair is to widen the check's range. One is a wall with too narrow a range; the other is a wall that cannot be built. WHAT THIS ROW DOES NOT CLAIM, because the carrier already argued the other side and it argued it well. `gunbc.rung_drop`'s own module note pre-registers this exact point: it records that splitting `authored` into the five fields was deliberately NOT attempted, because deciding per row which sentence is the reason and which the population is semantic rewriting with no oracle, and because doing it inside a relocation would make a content change indistinguishable from a move. That reasoning is sound and this row does not contest it -- the note is CITED rather than restated (DESIGN section 3). **WHAT THIS ROW ADDS IS THE CONSEQUENCE, WHICH CHANGES THE PRIORITY WITHOUT CHANGING THE ARGUMENT.** The note reasons from AUTHORING COST -- the split is expensive and unoracled, so defer it. The measured consequence is that while it is deferred, the obligation's own consistency check cannot be written, and a grain mismatch of exactly the kind 4b(3) warns about survived in a load-bearing row until an unrelated refusal exposed it. Before it, the change is dozens of edits to one file with no reviewable unit; after it, each row is an independent diff. RECOGNITION RULE, stated to generalise past this carrier: **for any rule that NAMES the facts a declaration must carry, count how many of those names are FIELDS.** If the count is zero and the rule also supplies a review tell, the tell is unmechanisable by construction and the rule is enforced by attention alone. The tell that you are looking at an instance rather than at ordinary prose is a governing document that enumerates required facts, plus a carrier whose record shape does not mention any of them, plus a review instruction phrased as something a reader should NOTICE. The sibling is the existence proof that the fields are authorable. **A BOUND ON THE TRIGGER BELOW, ESTABLISHED BY SOMEONE BUILDING THE WRONG VERSION OF IT (zesty-gull-732, 2026-09-03), recorded so this row's own remedy is not cited past its subject.** I proposed to that lane a neighbouring invariant -- every sibling identity a row NAMES resolves to a file -- and they implemented it, which falsified it twice. FIRST, the general form is not computable from `authored` at all: a backticked name in prose may be a sibling drop, a function, a data row in another module or a config key, and a prefix heuristic reported 21 UNRESOLVED across 10 rows, every one a false positive. A smuggled heuristic standing exactly where the structure is missing, which is an INSTANCE of this row rather than a check that could precede it. SECOND and sharper: narrowed to the one family a naming convention makes decidable, it ran GREEN on a tree carrying five siblings where main had eight. A whole-file restructure keeps its whole side, so the trigger TEXT and the sibling ROWS are lost together and the family stays internally consistent while being three guarantees short. **A self-consistency check cannot discriminate a CONSISTENT STALE COPY** -- green before the loss, green after it, green on the merged tree -- so it would be cited as coverage for a class it never touches. WHAT THAT DOES AND DOES NOT BOUND. It does NOT weaken the trigger below, whose subject is the grain relation WITHIN one row -- does this row's trigger population span this row's loss population -- which is decidable once both are fields and is exactly what no reader reliably performs. It DOES establish that such a join is confined to one tree, so it can never detect rows LOST BETWEEN TREES; that defect lives in the relationship between two versions and is invisible to anything reading one. Those are different subjects with different oracles, the second being an external comparison against another tree, and this row claims only the first. The general lesson is the cheaper one: the way that limit was found was by BUILDING the check and running it against the tree it was supposed to refuse. A proposed invariant that has not been run against its own discriminating red is a specification, and DESIGN section 5 says so. I proposed it without doing that; they did it, and the proposal did not survive. RUNG FOUND AT: outside the ladder. The failure is silent by construction -- a mismatched row is well-formed, projects cleanly, and passes every gate, because nothing reads inside the field. CEILING: 3, structurally guaranteed. Once previous rung, temporary rung, reason, population and trigger are typed, 'the trigger's population spans the loss population' is a decidable join at identity grain, and a row failing it need not be `Accepted`. Rung 4 is not attainable while a population is a list of names an author writes, since a wrong name is still spellable. Typing the fields WITHOUT that consumer does not discharge this: the sibling carrier shows fields plus consumers is the shape that works, and fields alone would leave the same tell unmechanised in a nicer record. Nor does the one-row-per-file split discharge it -- that removes the CONFLICT grain and leaves every fact still inside one prose field, which is how this row is known to be a distinct subject from gunbc#10197 rather than a restatement of it.)

RECOGNITION RULE: A RESIDUAL REFUSAL DOES NOT PROTECT A CLASSIFIER WHOSE ACCEPTED BUCKET IS DEFINED BY ABSENCE; A NEW KIND CAN SILENTLY RESEMBLE THE DEFAULT.
